Fixed 2026-11-16 in part (mr-mantle 9f62d658, mr-pass a40fb83): the invoice payment's and an event registration's return key ride as `returnToken` (`Portal.js`, `EventRegistrationPayments.js` `returnUrl`; InvoicePay reads the new name first and `key` for links sent before), and the authority's `getPaymentResult` takes `returnToken` (`returnKey`, still read, is a secret name since SEC-PSA-09); tests `AccountingPortals.spec.js`, `EventRegistrations.spec.js`, client `PortalHttp`. Fixed 2026-11-16 (the rest; mr-mantle a37472c4, mr-pass aee6ef5), the owner's call: the return key reads only its payment attempt's state. `Portal.js` `getPaymentResult` with the gateway return's reference (`returnToken`, or `returnKey`) answers only `{attemptId, state}` (paid, declined, pending or failed): no amount, date, payment reference, provider page or invoice, which need the pay link or a signed-in contact; PASS `Billing/InvoicePay.xml` reads the attempt with the link (the page's or the session's) or the sign-in first, and otherwise shows the state alone with "Open the link in your invoice email or sign in to see the invoice". Tests mr-mantle `AccountingPortals.spec.js` (the reference reads `attemptId,state` and no invoice), mr-pass `Billing/Portals.spec.js`, client `PortalHttp`.
- **SEC-PSS-28 (Low; reported (confirmed in code; a helper reader's item, not checked by the slice's reviewer); fixed). The public case form takes the typed email as the sender.** the address is matched to a party and, when it is a served customer's or its contact's, a case is made for that customer with no verification of the sender, and the route's acknowledgement is mailed to that address (up to 5 an hour per address and queue). Staff then see a case filed by the customer. Under the unknown-sender rule `SusIgnore` nothing is kept for an unknown address, so the sixth post within an hour answers "You sent several requests just now" only for an address already known, which tells a customer's address from an unknown one. A queue's hourly limit (30 by default) is shared by all senders, so one sender can close the form for the hour. Where: `request/SupportPortal.js:767-804`, `request/Channels.js:720-763`, `request/RequestServices.xml:887-921`. Fix: mark a form-filed case's sender unverified for staff, send no case link in its acknowledgement, and answer the neutral receipt when the address limit is reached. Fixed 2026-11-16 in part (mr-mantle a2ecc113): `create#IntakeRequest` answers a queue form's address past its five an hour as received, makes nothing and logs it (as the honeypot), so the answer no longer tells a known address from one the queue ignores; a project's form keeps its refusal; test `SupportChannels.spec.js` "an address past its hourly requests through a queue's form gets the neutral receipt". Refuted in part: `send#IntakeConfirmation` sends no case link or token. Fixed 2026-11-16 (the rest; mr-mantle b267c1cc, mr-boss f19074b5), the owner's call: a form-filed case shows "sender not verified" until a reply from that address or a sign-in. A request a queue's public form files (`create#IntakeRequest`, `Channels.js` `formRequest`) has `Request.senderVerified` N (a new audited field); `SupportCases.js` `confirmSender` sets Y when a reply from the typed address joins it (mail intake, not an automatic message) or when its filer opens it signed in (`SupportPortal.js` `caseFor`; a link's holder or another reader does not), a failure to clear being logged and never failing the reply or the read; Customer Service's CaseDetail shows "Sender not verified: the address was typed into a public form" (`Cases.js` `caseDetail` `senderUnverified`). Tests mr-mantle `SupportChannels.spec.js` "a case a queue's form filed is marked sender not verified until a reply comes from its address", `SupportPortal.spec.js` "a form-filed case's typed address is confirmed by its filer opening it signed in, not by another reader", mr-boss `CustomerServiceCases.spec.js`. Outside the call and not done: per-requester limits on the form (a queue's 30 an hour is shared by all senders).
- **SEC-PSS-43 (Low; reported (confirmed in code; a helper reader's item, not checked by the slice's reviewer); fixed). Redeeming an invitation reactivates a member the site's staff suspended or removed.** The only check on an existing membership is its role. A row in state `suspended` or `ended` is rewritten to `active`. joinSite refuses the same account (`Joining is not permitted`). The invitation may come from another actor than the site's owner: an internal user inviting a contact to Billing, Supplier, Support or Sales (`ExternalAccess.js` invite). Where: `service/mantle/forum/Forum.js` redeemInvitation (starts 2323). Fix: Refuse redemption (or give only the contact grant) when the existing membership is suspended or was ended by staff, as joinSite does. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-mantle b619835d): `Forum.js` `redeemInvitation` refuses ("Joining is not permitted") when the account's membership is suspended or was ended by someone other than the member, as `joinSite` does, and the invitation stays unredeemed; a member who left joins again; test `MailingLists.spec.js` "an invitation does not bring back a member staff suspended or removed".
- **SEC-PSS-44 (Low; reported (confirmed in code; a helper reader's item, not checked by the slice's reviewer); fixed). A list link's token does not expire by default, is not single use, has no lookup limit and also subscribes.** The token reads the organization's name, the masked address and the names and states of the party's lists of that organization, and can set `subscribed` true on any of those lists the party left, recorded as a consent grant with source `PcsEmailLink`. Use marks `usedDate` and nothing else. The lookups are not counted against the requester, unlike `ExternalAccess.js` `tokenCapability` (100 in ten minutes); the token's 32 random characters make guessing impractical, so the limit is hardening. Where: `service/mantle/party/ConsentServices.xml` create#UnsubscribeToken (starts 1289), resolve#UnsubscribeToken (1428), apply#UnsubscribePreferences (1506); `service/mantle/public/AccountHub.js:194-215`, `:280-303`. Fix: Give every token an expiry at creation, let a token only unsubscribe or undo a change made with that same token, and count token lookups as the other link kinds do. Fixed 2026-11-16 in part (mr-mantle c71f2205): `resolve#UnsubscribeToken` and `apply#UnsubscribePreferences` count each lookup against the requester first (`list-token:<requester>`, 100 in ten minutes), good token or not; test `Consent.spec.js` "list link lookups count against the requester first". Fixed 2026-11-16 (the rest; mr-mantle 77e79550, mr-pass 5742106), the owner's call: list link tokens expire after a year by default (a list setting), and a link only unsubscribes and can undo that within a day. `create#UnsubscribeToken` gives every list link a thruDate (the days asked, else the list's new `ContactList.linkValidDays`, 1 to 3650, else 365); a link unsubscribes as before, keeps the lists it unsubscribed on its `ContactListToken` row (`unsubscribedListIds`, `unsubscribedDate`; `ListLinks.js`), and subscribes the party again only to one of those within a day; any other subscription by a link, a Marketing topic too (`AccountHub.js` `storeMarketing`), is skipped (`apply#UnsubscribePreferences` `failedContactListIds`, `signInRequired`) and needs the preference page's sign-in, which PASS's page shows as "Sign in to subscribe" (`resolve#UnsubscribeToken` `undoable`, `get#EmailPreferences` `canSubscribe`). Links sent before keep no expiry. Tests mr-mantle `Consent.spec.js` (a year by default, the list's days; a list the link did not unsubscribe is not subscribed again; the undo within a day and not after), `MarketingSubscriptions.spec.js`.
- **SEC-PSS-45 (Low; reported (likely; a helper reader's item, not checked by the slice's reviewer); fixed). PASS's one-click unsubscribe is not a confirmed unsubscribe on a list that asks confirmation.** The operation calls apply#UnsubscribePreferences with the token's list set to not subscribed. That path calls set#ContactListPartySubscription without `confirmed`, so a list with an unsubscribe verification email goes to `CLPT_UNSUBS_PENDING` and a verification message is sent, where unsubscribe#ContactListParty with `listUnsubscribe=One-Click` confirms at once. The recipient stays on the list until they follow a second link. Where: `service/mantle/public/AccountHub.js:360-368`; `service/mantle/party/ConsentServices.xml` apply#UnsubscribePreferences, set#ContactListPartySubscription (716). Fix: Have oneClick call unsubscribe#ContactListParty with the token and `listUnsubscribe: 'One-Click'`. Fixed 2026-11-17 (mr-mantle 5b3131b2): `oneClick` calls `unsubscribe#ContactListParty` with the token, the token's list and `listUnsubscribe: 'One-Click'`, which confirms at once (`AccountHub.js`). Spec `MarketingSubscriptions` "SEC-PSS-45: PASS's one-click unsubscribe confirms at once on a list that asks unsubscribe confirmation".
- **SEC-PSS-62 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). A theme may hold a `<meta>` element and links to any host.** The layout is the whole HTML document and `meta` is not a denied element (only the `http-equiv` attribute is), so `<meta name="referrer" content="unsafe-url">` passes the source and output checks. If a browser lets it replace the `Referrer-Policy: same-origin` header, a visitor who follows a theme link to another host (`a href` may be any `http` or `https` address) sends the page address, which on some Shop pages carries a bearer value in the query (`Checkout/return?ref=…&key=…`, `Recover?token=…`). Where: crates/moqui-core/src/script/theme/markup.rs:124 (DENIED_ELEMENTS), :175 (DENIED_ATTRIBUTES), :628 (check_url, navigation). Fix: Allow `meta` only with an allow-listed `name` (`viewport`, `description`, `robots`, `theme-color`) or `charset`, and refuse `name="referrer"`. Fixed 2026-11-17 (commit ab82a7bbd): a theme's `<meta>` is a `charset`, a `name` of `viewport`, `description`, `keywords`, `author`, `robots`, `googlebot`, `theme-color`, `color-scheme`, `application-name`, `generator` or `format-detection` or a `twitter:` one, an Open Graph `property` (`og:`, `article:`, `product:` and the like) or `itemprop`; any other, `name="referrer"` among them, is `unsafe-markup` (`markup.rs` `META_NAMES`, `meta_allowed`). Test `script::theme::tests::a_theme_meta_is_a_charset_a_listed_name_or_a_card_property`.
- **SEC-PSS-65 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). `PortalMedia.sniff` reads the whole staged upload into memory to compare eight bytes.** Each accepted upload of up to 10 MiB is read whole into the script's memory (`readAllBytes`) only to take its first eight bytes. Several at once multiply that on the authority. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/PortalMedia.js:20. Fix: Read only the first eight bytes of the stream. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-mantle 40e940cf): `PortalMedia.js` `sniff` reads the staged file's first eight bytes from the stream (`read(0, 8)`), not the whole file; test `SupportPortal.spec.js` "a staged file's type is read from its first eight bytes alone" (it guards the results; no spec measures memory).
- **SEC-PSS-66 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). `check()` skips the organization comparison when its caller names none, and the intake form's check names none.** `check()` compares the capability's organization only when `p.organizationPartyId` is given. `formTarget` calls it with the record type, scope and token only, so at that check a queue form token of one organization is accepted whatever organization the site serves. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/ExternalAccess.js:386; runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/Channels.js:694. Fix: Pass the mount's organization to `check()` in `formTarget`, or make `check()` refuse a token lookup with no organization. Fixed 2026-11-17 (commit 2a53b8a29, mr-mantle fe17e3c1): `formTarget(token, organizationPartyId)` passes the organization a site's form serves to `check()`, so a queue's token of another organization is refused at the token check itself; the PASS Support mount's `intakeCase` gives its organization there and to `create#IntakeRequest`, which takes `organizationPartyId` and then accepts no project token (a project's form is never a site's). The business host's `/r/<token>` form serves no one organization, so its token alone still names the queue. Test mr-mantle `request/SupportChannels.spec.js` (a site of the other organization refused, its own taken).
- **SEC-PSS-67 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). Shop's address and card setup operations have no per-requester limit.** None of these functions calls the requester throttle the cart, checkout, review and download operations call. Each `beginPaymentMethodSetup` calls the store gateway's hosted setup service and each `storeAddress` may call the carrier's address validation, so one account can drive outbound gateway and carrier calls without bound. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/ShopCustomer.js:587, :751, :779. Fix: Count these posts against the requester as the other Shop writes do. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-mantle 5c4906ae): `ShopCustomer.js` `storeAddress`, `beginPaymentMethodSetup` and `finishPaymentMethodSetup` count against the requester first (`ExternalAccess.js` `throttle`, `shop-account`, 30 in ten minutes); test `ShopCustomer.spec.js` "an account's address and card setup posts count against its requester".
- **SEC-PSS-07 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Internal step and job services are `anonymous-all`.** These services check no caller. `ShopCheckoutServices.run#Step` with step `freeze` takes a checkout id and a `returnKeyDigest` from its input; `remove#ReviewImageFile` deletes a file at a given location (only under the reviews root, with no `..`, and only when no image row names it). They depend on `allow-remote="false"` and on there being no route that calls a service by a name from the request. Where: `M/order/ShopCheckoutServices.xml:167` (`run#Step`), `:181`, `:201`, `:216`; `M/order/ShopOperationsServices.xml:8-67`; `M/product/ShopSubscriptionServices.xml:83`, `:98`; `M/product/StoreMerchandisingServices.xml:507`, `:548`; `M/public/AccountHubServices.xml:84`, `:105`. Fix: Declare the steps `authenticate="false"` only where a job needs it and have each step refuse a call that did not come from its own service (a marker in the context), as `PaymentAttempts.asAttempt` does for postings.
- **SEC-PSS-46 (Info; reported (confirmed in code; a helper reader's item, not checked by the slice's reviewer)). The neutral limits of public subscription and registration can be used to shut out a site or an address.** The per-site counters (1,000 and 500 an hour) and the per-address counters (5 an hour) are shared by all requesters. Thirty requests in ten minutes from each of a few addresses fill a site's hour, after which every visitor gets the receipt and nothing is kept (subscription) or a refusal (registration); five requests naming a victim's address stop that address's own request for the hour. Where: `service/mantle/marketing/MarketingPortal.js` requestSubscription (starts 419), reserveRegistration (starts 1058). Fix: None needed if accepted; otherwise alert on a site counter reaching its limit.
- **SEC-PSS-63 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). The theme expression check does not see a property name built at run time.** A computed member is checked only when its property is a string literal or a template literal with no expression, so `x["con" + "structor"]` passes the name deny list. What stops it is the locked sandbox (a frozen global, the view snapshot only) and the output check, not this check. Where: crates/moqui-core/src/script/theme/expr.rs:140. Fix: Refuse a computed member whose property is not a literal string or number, or remove `Function` and `eval` from the locked realm.
- **SEC-PSS-64 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). The checkout relay writes the answered form address without the host check the card relay makes.** `relayPage` of `ShopCheckout.js` puts `form.formUrl` into the form's `action` and submits it with the payment token; `relayPage` of `ShopAccount.js` first requires `https://(accept|test).authorize.net/`. The guard that exists is the route's CSP `form-action`, which names the same two origins. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-pass/screen/Shop/ShopCheckout.js:891; runtime/components/addon/mr-pass/screen/Shop/ShopAccount.js:832. Fix: Make the same host check in `ShopCheckout.js` `relayPage` before writing the form.
- **SEC-PSS-84 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A checkout's reference and return key get a fresh hosted payment form, with no test of how the call came and no requester count, and each form call first searches the provider's transaction lists.** `getCheckoutPaymentForm` reaches the checkout through `reach`, which accepts a reference with its key (`via` "reference") as well as the cart, and then checks only the attempt's status and amount, never `via`; Marketing's twin refuses its return key (`MarketingPortal.js:1191` `if (via === "return") unavailable()`). So the key, which reads a minimal view, also continues the payment: the result page's "Continue to the payment page" opens `PaymentRelay`, which gets a new Accept Hosted token for that payment and posts it. On that path no lookup is counted (the cart lookup returns before its throttle when no token is sent, `ShopCart.js:112-114`, and `checkoutOfReference` has none), and each form call lists the merchant's unsettled transactions twice, the settled batches of the last day and each batch's transactions (up to five pages of 1,000 each), then asks for a token and moves `hostedFormDate`, which keeps the payment waiting; a result read is paced to one lookup a second for each attempt and makes a similar set of list requests. The calls spend the merchant's Authorize.Net API allowance, which also carries its real payments; any visitor can make a pending checkout with a guest cart, and a requester may hold 30 new carts in ten minutes. Reached by anyone who holds a pending checkout's reference and key (the shopper who submitted it, or a visitor into whose session the PASS frontend's `Checkout/return` page put another shopper's pair from a link; that page is the PASS frontend area's entry from the same report), through `getCheckoutPaymentForm` and `getCheckoutPaymentResult` (`POST shop/checkout/payment/form` and `shop/checkout/result`, `script/PassRoutes.js:254-255`, both `TOKEN_WRITES`). Not confirmed: Authorize.Net's own API limits and what they do to the merchant's other calls; what its hosted page shows the payer; not run. Reports: round 3 `C3.md` (the `via` test) and `C8.md` (the provider searches). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/ShopCheckout.js:192-200`, `:214-221`, `:1437-1469`, `:1500-1533`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/gateway/AuthorizeNet.js:392-425`, `:949-999`, `:1006-1035`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/MarketingPortal.js:1187-1191`. Fix: answer the payment form only to the cart's holder, the placed cart's session or the signed-in buyer (`via` "cart", "placedCart" or "account"), as Marketing does, so the reference and key read the state alone; count `getCheckoutPaymentForm` and `getCheckoutPaymentResult` against the requester, as the cart operations are, and pace the form per attempt (for example one every few seconds, and a bounded number per attempt).
- **SEC-PSS-85 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A guest uses a once-per-customer code or promotion on every order.** A guest's cart has no customer until `beginCheckout` makes a new guest party for it, so each guest checkout is a customer with no uses: `add#OrderPromoCode` with no customer allows the whole per-customer limit, and the placement's limit check counts per-customer uses only of that new party. A "first order" or "one per customer" code, or a promotion with `useLimitPerCustomer`, is taken again on each guest order with the same email and address; the per-code and per-promotion totals still hold. Reached by anonymous, through `applyPromoCode` and the checkout operations of a guest cart (`script/PassRoutes.js:236`, `:243-255`), in a store whose `accountMode` is not `account` (the default is `either`, `StoreCheckout.js:33`). Looked for and not found: a count by the checkout's contact email or address (`checkOrderPromotionLimits`, `StorePromotions.js:971-1060`); a refusal of per-customer codes for a guest. Report: round 3 `C8.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/ShopCheckout.js:601-619`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/PromotionServices.xml:338-355`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/StorePromotions.js:1039-1040`, `:40`, `:45`. Fix: count a guest checkout's per-customer uses by its normalized contact email at the freeze and the placement, or refuse a code or promotion with a per-customer limit for a guest checkout (as `requireParty` does) and say so in Commerce's code editor.
- **SEC-PSS-86 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A subscriber can make any number of off-session charges of the kept card at once, past the retry days.** `resume#Renewal` and `change#SubscriptionMethod` reopen a renewal whose retries ran out (`SrsFailed` for the current period) or that waits for a retry to a retry due now (`reopenStep`), and `advance` makes a new renewal payment and authorizes it at once as a merchant-initiated, stored-credential charge; a decline ends it `SrsFailed` again, which the next call reopens. The offer's retry days (1, 3, 7) bound the job, not these calls, and neither operation counts against the requester, so each call can send another merchant-initiated authorization of a card that is being declined, which card networks limit for the merchant. Reached by a signed-in verified account, on its own contract, through `resumeRenewal` and `changeSubscriptionMethod` (`POST shop/subscriptions/resume` and `shop/subscriptions/method`, `script/PassRoutes.js:291`, `:293`; not `TOKEN_WRITES`, so with a delegation or the business-host session's token). Looked for and not found: a `throttle` call in `ShopSubscriptions.js`; a limit of reopenings per period (`attemptCount` only decides `SrsFailed`, `:936-937`). Report: round 3 `C8.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/ShopSubscriptions.js:1199-1208`, `:1112-1134`, `:1166-1196`, `:983-984`, `:744-750`, `:811-909`. Fix: count `resumeRenewal` and `changeSubscriptionMethod` against the requester, and allow a customer's resumption a bounded number of charges a period (for example one a day after the retries are used).
- **SEC-PSS-87 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A forged public-form case is marked sender-verified when the owner of the typed address opens it (SEC-PSS-28's fix does not cover this).** The form's typed address is matched to a party, a user account's party first (`Channels.js:275-282`). For a served customer, or a contact of one, `formRequest` makes a case whose filer is that matched party and whose customer is the customer, marked `senderVerified` N, and `create#Request` makes the filer the case's Reporter and the customer its Customer, with the typer's subject, description and file (kept as a customer-shared file). When the address's owner opens the case signed in, `caseFor` calls `confirmSender` with the owner's party, which is the filer, and the case is marked verified, so staff see a case from the customer's address with no "Sender not verified" mark. SEC-PSS-28's fix ("until a reply from that address or a sign-in") assumes the filer is the person who typed the form; here the filer is whoever owns the typed address, and opening is not filing. Reached by anonymous with a queue's public form token through `createCustomerCase` (`POST support/cases`, `TOKEN_WRITES`), then the address's owner, signed in to the Support site, through `getCustomerCase`. Confirmed in code for the flag; that the case lists in the owner's MyCases (`SupportPortal.js:661-665`) is likely, as the after-commit `ensure#CaseProfile` of `entity/Request.eecas.xml` was not traced; what Customer Service's CaseDetail shows besides the flag was not read. Report: round 3 `C9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/SupportPortal.js:290-292`, `:814-816`, `:882`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/SupportCases.js:369-371`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/Channels.js:752-765`, `:275-282`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:51-56`. Fix: clear the flag only on a reply from the address, or on a sign-in only when the case was filed signed in; keep a form-filed case out of the matched customer's MyCases (or show it as filed with their address by a web form) until the address confirms it; keep a form's file as an internal file, not a customer-shared one.
- **SEC-PSS-88 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Anonymous reservations take every seat of a free event and keep a paid event's seats held, and each typed attendee address is mailed a ticket.** A reservation holds 1 to 10 places, limited to 10 reservations a requester in ten minutes, 500 a site an hour and 5 an hour per buyer address (any syntactically valid address). Completing it names the attendees, each a new person with the typed address; when every held place is free the places are confirmed at once, and each confirmed place's ticket (title, date, entry code, the join link of an online event) is mailed to the attendee's typed address, with no per-recipient limit. Confirmed places and unexpired holds count as taken seats (`EventRegistrations.js:237-239`), so one requester confirms about 600 free places an hour and a few take a free event's seats, and a paid event's seats can be kept held by renewing 15-minute holds. Reached by anonymous, through `reserveRegistration` then `completeRegistration` (both `TOKEN_WRITES`) on any site with a published, open offer. SEC-PSS-46 records the shared neutral limits, not this. Looked for and not found: an address check on attendees (`complete` takes `personInput` with `addressRequired` false); a per-attendee or per-event limit (only `ticket.perOrderLimit`, per reservation); a confirmation of the buyer's address before a free place is confirmed. Not confirmed: whether deployments run free public events at scale; the ledger's own send limits. Report: round 3 `C9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/MarketingPortal.js:1099-1168`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventRegistrationPlaces.js:331-333`, `:386`, `:441-498`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventRegistrationNotices.js:142-146`. Fix: confirm a free place only after the buyer's address confirms (the receipt's link), or cap confirmed places per buyer address and per requester per offer; limit tickets per recipient address.
- **SEC-PSS-89 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A registration's payment form and its return read ask the payment gateway on every call, and the signed-in buyer's calls count against nothing.** `getRegistrationPaymentForm` calls the gateway's hosted-form service (`AuthorizeNetServices.get#HostedPaymentForm`, "asked on each call and never stored") for a fresh token on each call while the payment is pending and its places held. `getRegistration` with `returned` set looks the payment up at the gateway on each call: the two-second gap between lookups applies only to the `page` mode, and `returned` is the client's. The capability path counts each lookup against the requester (100 in ten minutes, `ExternalAccess.js:348`); the account path, a signed-in account reaching its own reservation by `reservationId`, calls no throttle, so a buyer drives outbound calls on the organization's gateway account without bound, as SEC-PSS-67 found for Shop's card setup. Reached by the buyer: a signed-in account reaching its own reservation, or the capability's holder, through `getRegistrationPaymentForm` and `getRegistration` (POSTs, `TOKEN_WRITES`). Confirmed in code to the gateway services; the outbound HTTP call is likely (the Authorize.Net script was not read in this unit). Report: round 3 `C9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/MarketingPortal.js:936-968`, `:1189-1200`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventRegistrationPayments.js:400-437`, `:443-455`. Fix: count `getRegistrationPaymentForm`, `startRegistrationPayment` and `getRegistration` against the requester on every path, and keep the lookup gap for the `return` mode too (or take `returned` only with the return key).
- **SEC-PSS-90 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). A theme preview link keeps answering the draft after its maker loses the Commerce permission or the store.** `create#ThemePreview` checks the maker's permission and store (`ThemeAuthoring.js:1048-1058`); at each read `get#ThemePreviewPage` checks of the maker only that the account exists and its `disabled` is not `Y`, never its theme permissions, its store scope or `terminateDate`. A staff member removed from the store or the Commerce groups, or whose account was ended by date, keeps a working preview of the current draft, every file of it, until it expires twenty minutes after it was made. Reached by anyone holding the preview URL (`/preview/{token}` on the site's preview host), through `get#ThemePreviewPage` (`ThemeServices.xml:918`, `anonymous-all`), called in process by mr-pass `screen/Shop/ShopPreview.js`. Looked for and not found: a call of `themes.scopeWith` or `stores.storeOf` in `getThemePreviewPage`; a cleanup of a user's previews on a group or scope change (only the maker's own expired previews are removed, `:1088-1099`). Not confirmed: what the framework's `disabled` flag holds after an administrator ends a staff account (it may be set, which closes most cases). Report: round 3 `C7.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/public/ThemeAuthoring.js:1118-1132`, `:46`, `:1073`. Fix: check the maker as the scheduled publication job does (sign-in eligibility, `terminateDate`, and cheaply a current theme permission), or delete a user's previews when their Commerce access ends.
- **SEC-PSS-91 (Info; reported (likely)). `PublicTheme.packageRoot` is trusted as the theme package's location for reads and writes with authorization off.** The location is made once from the theme id (`rootOf`, `dbresource://mantle/public/theme/{themeId}`) and stored, and every later read and write takes the stored field: the draft is read and written there as the system (`draftFiles`, `putDraftFiles` inside `asSystem`), a release's files are read and checked there, and `getShopThemeAsset` answers `packageRoot + path` as the location mr-pass streams with authorization disabled (`screen/Shop/ShopTheme.js:396-405`). A row pointing elsewhere (`component://`, `file://`) would let a COMMERCE_THEME_CODE holder write theme-shaped files (`.json`, `.css`, `.svg`, `.html.fancy-locked`, `assets/*.png`) under that location as the system; it is the class of SEC-SCR-92 (a record field that names a location). Reached by whoever can write `mantle.public.PublicTheme.packageRoot` directly (a generic entity editor or a data import; no Mantle service writes it but `Themes.js:464` and `ThemeAuthoring.js:278`, each with `rootOf(themeId)`), then any theme editor of the store and any visitor of its Shop. Likely: the reads and writes are confirmed in code; which groups can write the entity through Tools or a data import was not traced, and no EECA or artifact authorization on the entity was found. Report: round 3 `C7.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/public/Themes.js:130`, `:214`, `:227-231`, `:260`, `:780`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/public/ThemeAuthoring.js:704`, `:987`. Fix: use `rootOf(theme.themeId)` everywhere and drop the field, or refuse a theme whose `packageRoot` is not `rootOf(themeId)`.
- **SEC-PSS-92 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). The public case form stages and scans its upload before the form's honeypot, field checks and limits.** After the token check and the operation's repeat answer, `createCustomerCase` writes the file (up to 10 MiB) under `pass@media-root` and passes it to the scanner; only then does `create#IntakeRequest` drop a filled honeypot, refuse missing names or a bad address, refuse the queue's 31st post of the hour and answer an address's sixth post with the neutral receipt, each time removing the staged file after its write and scan. A robot filling the honeypot or an address over its limit so still costs a write and a scan per post, 30 posts in ten minutes per requester; SEC-PSS-22's fix moved staging after the token check, but the token is public (it is on the NewCustomerCase page). Report: round 3 `C9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/SupportPortal.js:851-883`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:855-913`. Fix: run the honeypot, field and limit checks (a `check` mode of `create#IntakeRequest`) before staging.
- **SEC-PSS-93 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Checkout opt-in confirmation codes are looked up with no requester count.** `getEmailPreferences` and `storeEmailPreferences` with `confirm` (`TOKEN_WRITES`, anonymous) look the code up through `checkoutOfCode`. The code is `checkoutId + "." + ec.user.randomString(32)` (`StoreTopics.js:413`) with its digest kept, so guessing is impractical, but unlike a list link's token (counted since SEC-PSS-44, `list-token:`) each lookup is free; hardening only. Report: round 3 `C9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/public/StoreTopics.js:424-433`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/public/AccountHub.js:192-195`, `:289-292`. Fix: count `checkoutOfCode` against the requester as `resolve#UnsubscribeToken` does.
- **SEC-PSS-94 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). A registration's return key reads its payment amount and the provider's page, with no time bound.** Whoever holds the provider return address `/Register?ref=…&returnToken=…` (browser history, a shared screen) reads through `getRegistration` with `reference` and `key` the event's title and date, the state, the amount and, while the payment is pending, the gateway's redirect address or form flag, for as long as the reservation exists: the key's check compares the digest only. Billing's return key (SEC-PSS-27) reads only `{attemptId, state}`, and only for a day after settling (`Portal.js:372`, `:383-392`). Report: round 3 `C9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/MarketingPortal.js:948-953`, `:982-1000`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventRegistrationPayments.js:381-393`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentAttempts.js:105-110`. Fix: answer the return key only `{reference, state}` and stop it a day after the payment settles.

## Forum and Smithy

- **SEC-PFS-01 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). The framework's review-thread REST gives party ids and party names of a Smithy repository's commenters.** Smithy's own pages name an author by the site profile's handle and only when `profileShown` allows it, and its reads return no party id. The framework REST's thread read goes to `handle#GitReviewThread`, then `get#ReviewThreads`, which answers each comment's `fromPartyId` and an `authorName` built from `PartyDetail` (organization name, or first and last name, or the party id). The REST passes the list through unchanged. An internal user's legal name and every commenter's party id reach any account that views the repository. Where: `mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/GitServices.xml:1119, 1128, 1189`; `crates/moqui-git/src/service/rest.rs:943-947`. Fix: For a repository Smithy keeps, answer the site profile's handle and display name (as `profileShown` allows) and leave `fromPartyId` out of the REST answer. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-mantle 64dde822): for a repository Smithy keeps, `mantle.work.GitServices.handle#GitReviewThread` answers each comment with no `fromPartyId` and `authorName`/`authorHandle` from the site profile when `SmithyCore.authorShown` lets the caller see it, else empty; other repositories' are as before; test `SmithyMergeRequests.spec.js` "a line comment is a post with its anchor ..." (the REST read as a viewer). No framework change: the REST passes on what the service answers.
- **SEC-PFS-02 (Low; reported (likely); fixed). Staged forum uploads have no count or total size limit per member.** Each call writes up to 10 MiB under `pass_media_root` and a `ForumAttachment` row, and calls the scanner. Nothing in the operation counts the caller's staged files or bytes, and no daily counter is taken (`takeDaily` is used only for invitations). Files not attached to a post stay 7 days. Where: `mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:2494-2599`; `mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/MediaWorkerServices.xml:32`. Fix: Cap the staged files and bytes of one party (count the rows with no `communicationEventId`), and count uploads against the trust level's daily limits. Fixed 2026-11-17 (mr-mantle 7092a34e): `stageAttachment` refuses (`Tarpit`, `limitReached`) a member who has 20 files or 50 MiB staged in the site and not yet on a post (rows with no `communicationEventId` that have not expired), and counts each upload against the day's `uploads` of the member's trust level (`TRUST_EFFECTS`: new 10, basic 50, member 200, regular 400), before anything is written. Test mr-mantle `SmithyEdit.spec.js` "a member's staged files and the day's uploads are capped".
- **SEC-PFS-62 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). The webhook secret is inside the stored hash of `storeWebhook`'s receipt.** `receipt(p, ...)` hashes the whole input, and `p` still holds `secret` and the URL. The sibling operations take the secret out first (`storeCiSecret` `{...p, value: null}`, `storeMirror` `mirrorReceipt`, `addSshKey` and `createAccessToken` drop `password` and `code`), and `SmithyImport.js` says of a receipt "no trace of the secret, not even its hash". A short webhook secret can be guessed offline against that hash, since every other field of the input is known from the webhook row. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyGit.js:225`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:510-518`. Fix: Call `receipt({...p, secret: undefined}, ...)` as `storeMirror` does. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-mantle d301022d): `SmithyGit.js` `storeWebhook` makes its operation receipt from the input without the secret, as `storeMirror` does, so `ForumOperationReceipt.payloadHash` holds no trace of it (a retry with only another secret now replays the first answer); test `SmithyRepositorySettings.spec.js` "a Maintainer keeps the webhooks ...". Receipts made before keep their hash until they expire.
- **SEC-PFS-63 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). For an account with no password, "sign in again" is any sign-in of the account in the last 15 minutes.** The re-authentication before a key or token is made counts `UserLoginHistory` rows of the account, not of this session. A session taken within 15 minutes of its sign-in, or any old session while the owner signs in elsewhere, passes and gets a credential that outlives the session by up to 366 days. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyGit.js:290-306`. Fix: Ask for this session's own sign-in time (or a fresh provider sign-in with `max_age`), not the account's latest. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-mantle 8a38e777, 0f7a6233; mr-pass bb997af; framework spec commit 2ba2bbe0e): `SmithyGit.js` `reauthenticate`, for an account with no password, takes through a remote frontend the visitor's own sign-in, its delegation's issue (`PassAuthority.js` `passSignedInAt`), and otherwise this session's own (`check#RecentAuth`, 300 seconds: a password, a second factor's code or a provider sign-in with its `auth_time`), never the account's login history; mr-pass `startSso` asks the provider for a fresh sign-in (`reauth: true`) when an account is already signed in. Tests `SmithyGitAccess.spec.js` "an account with no password to give again signs in again instead" (an old delegation, and a fresh history row alone, are refused), `RecentAuth.spec.js` "a PASS public sign-in (start#Session) is the session's recent sign-in; a delegation's internal login is not (SEC-PFS-63)". An OAuth (not OpenID) provider's sign-in counts as recent only when asked as a re-authentication.
- **SEC-PFS-64 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). Mail is taken as authenticated on a DKIM pass for any parent of the From domain.** `aligned` accepts `header.d` equal to the From domain or any suffix of it at a label boundary, not the organizational domain, and DMARC need not pass. Mail with `From: member@team.host.example` signed by `d=host.example` is taken as that member's when the member's account has that verified address, and is posted as the member through `publishPost`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:5113-5143`. Fix: Take DMARC pass alone as the proof, or align DKIM on the organizational domain by the public suffix list. Fixed 2026-11-17 (mr-mantle 7092a34e): `gatewayAuthenticated` takes a DKIM pass only for the From domain itself (strict alignment); a parent's signature (`d=host.example` for `team.host.example`) no longer counts, and the gateway's DMARC pass, which aligns on the organizational domain by the public suffix list, still does. Test mr-mantle `MailingLists.spec.js` "only the trusted gateway's Authentication-Results authenticate a sender" (`parentDkim`, `subDmarc`).
- **SEC-PFS-03 (Info; reported (to check)). Hook and worker services are `anonymous-all` with the user named in a parameter.** `check#Push`, `check#LfsUpload`, `check#Outbound`, `check#RestWrite`, `add#HostingOffer`, `check#AuthorSigned`, `read#Component`, `check#Run` and `get#RunUrl` take `repositoryId`, `userId` or `triggeredByUserId` as plain parameters and need no authentication or authorization. `check#Run` also counts a day's pipeline run for the user it is given (`SmithyPipelines.js:323-326`). They rely on never being reachable from a request. Where: `mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyGitServices.xml:9, 39, 53, 72, 89, 102, 117`; `SmithyPipelineServices.xml:176, 212`; `mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/DeliveryServices.xml:242`. Fix: Declare them `authenticate="false"` with a check that authorization is disabled by the caller, as the framework's `queue#PushRuns` does, or leave a note in the map that they must stay unrouted.
- **SEC-PFS-65 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). `findWebhooks` answers a webhook's whole URL, which may hold a credential.** A webhook URL is stored and answered whole. The code itself notes that it may carry a credential (a token in the query or user info), and keeps it out of the audit row for that reason, while mirrors and environments refuse a URL with a user or password and answer `hasCredential` only. One Maintainer reads the token another put in a webhook URL. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyGit.js:185-200, 216`. Fix: Refuse user info in a webhook URL as `storeMirror` does, and answer the query string masked.
- **SEC-PFS-86 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An edited post's earlier text stays readable to every reader of the post, and no edit, a moderator's included, withdraws it.** Every revision `publishPost` and `updatePost` write is stored with `publicHistoryAllowed: 'Y'`, and `historyAccess` shows a revision to any reader of the post when the flag is `Y`, so `getPostHistory` and `comparePostRevisions` (a post's History link, `Topics/PostHistory?postId=...&fromRevision=1&toRevision=2`) show every revision to every reader, an anonymous visitor of a public category included. When a moderator edits an address, a phone number or abuse out of a post, or an author removes a secret pasted by mistake, the removed text stays public, and on a wiki post any eligible member may restore it (`restorePostRevision`). Only the import and the retention purge of a deleted or rejected post set the flag to `N`, and the first post of a topic cannot be deleted alone; Smithy's issue text is kept the same way (`SmithyIssues.js:630`), not traced. Reports: round 3 `C2.md`, `C4.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:1472-1481`, `:1648-1660`, `:4415-4505`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumOperations.js:483-492`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-pass/screen/Shared/View.js:1249-1269`, `:1706-1718`. Fix: Let a staff edit (and an author's) mark the earlier revisions hidden (`publicHistoryAllowed: 'N'`), or give staff a "hide this revision" action; `permitted` then keeps them to the author and staff, and `restorePostRevision` already refuses a hidden one to everyone else.
- **SEC-PFS-87 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). In local mode a forum attachment is answered with its stored type and no sandbox, and an imported one's type and name come from the source file name (SEC-PSF-08's fix does not reach `Facade.download`).** An upload is held to six inert types (`stageAttachment`), but the Discourse import sets `mimeType` from `contentTypeOf(original_filename)`, `filename` as the source gave it and `byteSize` from the dump, so `x.js` is stored as `application/javascript` (or a file as `text/html` or `image/svg+xml`). In local mode (`pass@backend-url` empty) `Facade.download` sets that type as it is, with `nosniff` and `Content-Disposition: attachment` but without the inert-type rule and the sandbox policy that `downloadFile` and the remote authority (`servedType`) apply since SEC-PSF-08's fix; `downloadExport` has the same shape. `attachment` keeps a browser from showing the file on a navigation (`C5.md`: no script runs on the site's origin as far as read), not a `<script src>` from loading it, so such a file is a same-origin script the shell's `script-src 'self'` allows: alone it does nothing, and with any markup injection on the site it is the condition SEC-PSF-08 removed for Smithy's release files. Reached by any reader of a topic with an imported attachment (`.../download?attachmentId=`), the file being what the imported community's members uploaded, in an archive an operator registers; not confirmed whether Discourse archives carry script-typed uploads or what the scanner refuses. `C1.md` reports the same gap in `download` and `downloadExport` for the PASS frontend, where today only inert types reach them. Reports: round 3 `C2.md`, `C5.md`. Unit C1 (`round3/C1.md`) found the same for the frontend's attachment and export downloads (`MP/service/pass/Facade.js:83`, `:112`). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-pass/service/pass/Facade.js:22-35`, `:75-105`, `:108-136`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumImport.js:1534-1586`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/script/PassAuthority.js:474-486`. Fix: Call `servedType` and `sandboxed` in `Facade.download` and `downloadExport` as `downloadFile` does; hold an imported attachment to `stageAttachment`'s type list and file name rule (anything else stored as `application/octet-stream`, or left out and counted), and take its size from the file.
- **SEC-PFS-88 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). `endSanction` asks no rank, so staff lift a silence on themselves or a sanction a higher rank put on a peer.** `setSanction` refuses a sanction on oneself and asks a rank above the target's; `endSanction` asks only `staff()` of the sanction's category, which refuses a suspended caller but not a silenced one. So the owner silences moderator M and M ends it, and moderator M2 ends a suspension the owner put on moderator M1. Reached by a site moderator, a list owner or moderator, or a bound scope's Owner, through `POST moderation/sanctions/{sanctionId}/end` with the id and revision from `GET moderation/members/{targetPartyId}`; whether the pages offer the action was not read. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumModeration.js:913-930`, `:967-996`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:656-674`. Fix: Refuse ending a sanction of the caller's own, and call `requireHigherRank(siteId, sanction.contactListId, sanction.partyId)`.
- **SEC-PFS-89 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A moderator of one list reads any site member's membership, trust and reading statistics.** `getMember` narrows only a bound scope's Owner (`scopeOwnerOnly`) to its scope; a list's owner or moderator is `staffModerate` and gets, for any party of the site, the membership (state, role, joined) and the member state (trust level and reason, approved contributions, last post, topics entered, posts read, reading seconds, visit days). The target need not be on that list, though `ForumCore.js:324-325` says a member's activity across the site is not a scope's to read. Reached by a list owner or moderator of any one category through `GET moderation/members/{targetPartyId}?contactListId=<their category>`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumModeration.js:997-1065`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:230-237`, `:324-326`. Fix: Answer a caller that is category staff and not site staff as the `scopeOwnerOnly` branch does.
- **SEC-PFS-90 (Low; reported (likely)). A site owner's regex rule backtracks until the deadline on every post of the site and on each `testRule`.** `validRule` refuses a quantified group that holds `+`, `*` or `}`, and back references; alternation and optional forms pass (`(a|a)*$`, `(a?){30}a{30}`, `.*.*.*.*.*!`, at most 200 characters). `applyRules` runs up to 200 rules on the plain text plus the HTML of a post (up to twice 100,000 characters), and `testRule` runs a pattern on a sample with no count; the engine is quickjs-oxide's backtracking `regexp`, with an interrupt poll every 10,000 steps and no step limit of its own (round 3 `B9.md`). Each such match holds a shared request worker until the transaction's deadline. Reached by a site owner through `POST rules` and `POST rules/test`, then by every post, edit and mail-in post of the site; not confirmed that the RegExp builtin passes the host interrupt to the executor, nor the transaction timeout's value. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumModeration.js:1299-1319`, `:1378-1389`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:864-873`, `:1369-1394`. Fix: Match rules with a linear-time engine (Rust `regex` through a host member) or a small step budget, and count `testRule` calls.
- **SEC-PFS-91 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A site owner reads the name of any product and the path of any wiki page of the host by linking it to a topic, and a published page's path is shown to every reader.** `linkDiscussion` takes `sourceType: 'product'` with any `productId` of any organization and `'article'` with any `wikiPageId` of any space, and checks only that the row exists. `getTopicLinks` then answers the card's title (`productName`, `pagePath`) to a moderator whether or not the product or page is the site's, and to every reader of the topic when the page has a published version and no subscription binding (`articleReadable` asks nothing of the page's space). A wrong id answers Not Found, so ids can be probed, and an internal page's title can be shown on a public topic. Reports: round 3 `C4.md`, `C5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumIntegration.js:404-436`, `:687-703`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:342-359`. Fix: Accept an article only of the site's `publishingWikiSpaceId` (or a space the caller administers) and a product only of the store of the site's `shop` mount (`productPublic(siteId, productId)`), in `linkDiscussion` and in `articleReadable`.
- **SEC-PFS-92 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A signed-in account stores drafts of any size and number on any published site, and none expires.** `storeDraft` asks a visible site, a party and, for an existing draft, its owner; it checks no length and no membership and counts nothing, and with no `draftId` each call makes a row. Nothing deletes a `ForumDraft` but export, erasure and merges, and no retention class covers drafts; `previewPost` renders and cleans a body of any length. The only bound is the transport's body cap, as the map gives it. Reached by any account with a party, member of the site or not, through `POST me/drafts` and `POST preview`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:1238-1280`, `:1314-1320`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumOperations.js:49-56`. Fix: The post length and title checks in both operations, an active membership, a cap per party and site, and expiry of untouched drafts.
- **SEC-PFS-93 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Exports and reports have no count, and one member can fill the operations queue every site shares.** Each personal export request makes a `ForumDataRequest`, a `ForumExport` and a pending `ForumJobRun`, and later a zip kept 7 days; only an erasure is limited to one open request, and no day's count is taken. The dispatcher takes the 50 oldest pending `op-%` runs of all sites a minute, so a burst from one site delays every site's erasures, retention and imports (the missing count confirmed, the delay likely). Reached by any member (`POST me/data-requests` with `kind: 'export'`), a category's staff (`POST reports/exports`) and a site owner (`POST operations`). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumOperations.js:288-335`, `:872-919`, `:1130-1141`, `:1458-1554`. Fix: One open personal export per member and site, a day's count for report exports, and dispatch fair across sites.
- **SEC-PFS-94 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A site owner sends list invitation mail to any number of addresses.** `inviteListMembers` takes 500 addresses a call, and site owners and operators skip both day's limits; every new address gets the site's mail with the owner's list and site names. Only one open invitation per address and list holds, and an owner can make lists. Reached by a site owner or operator through `POST categories/{contactListId}/list-invitations`. Reports: round 3 `C4.md`, `C5.md` (as part of its sender item). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumLists.js:954-1037`. Fix: A per-site day's cap that holds for owners, operators exempt.
- **SEC-PFS-95 (Low; reported (likely)). A site owner makes active again a member who left the site.** `leaveSite` sets the membership `ended`, records the member as who ended it, and keeps the member's delivery preferences; `setMembership` sets any state on any row without looking at who ended it, so the member is listed, open to messages and mailed by the old watch preferences again, without consent. `joinSite` keeps the reverse rule for a staff removal. The mail that follows was traced only through `categoryAccess`. Reached by a site owner through `PATCH members/{targetPartyId}` with `state: 'active'`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:404-414`, `:429-443`, `:470-497`. Fix: Refuse `active` or `pending` for a membership its member ended.
- **SEC-PFS-96 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A site owner suspends or removes the other owners without signing in again, though a role change asks it.** `setSiteRole` and a category role change ask for the password and code again (`login#UserAccount`); `setMembership`, which takes an owner's rights away as surely, asks only `site(p.siteId, true)` and `protectOwner`, which the caller satisfies. Reached by a site owner, or a stolen session or delegation of one, through `PATCH members/{targetPartyId}` with `suspended` or `ended`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:470-497`, `:498-514`, `:3213-3233`. Fix: Ask the same re-authentication when the target is an owner.
- **SEC-PFS-97 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). `joinCategory` adds a join request on every call and does not test a suspension.** `listMember` finds only an accepted row, so in a category that asks approval each call writes one more `CLPT_PENDING` row, and the moderators get them all (`findCategoryMembers`). The member test is the membership's state alone, not its dates or a site suspension. Reached by any signed-in member through `POST categories/{contactListId}/membership`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:3122-3147`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:171-185`. Fix: Answer an existing current row, pending included, and test the member as `site()` does.
- **SEC-PFS-98 (Low; reported (likely)). One anonymous `GET tags` may evaluate 100,000 topics.** `findTags` reads, for each of up to 100 tags, up to 1,000 tagged topics and runs each through `topic()`, several queries each. There is no stored count and no rate limit in `PassApi.js`. Reached by an anonymous visitor of a published site, `GET tags?limit=100`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:3804-3860`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:439`. Fix: Keep a count per tag of its public topics, or count in one query, and cap the rows across tags.
- **SEC-PFS-99 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Any site owner sends the forum's mail from any registered sender address and template, another site's included.** `storeMailSettings` takes a sender of `pass@sender-addresses` and a template of `pass@mail-template-ids` whose `fromAddress` is that sender; both lists are the deployment's, not the site's. On a deployment with several sites, each with its own sender, site B's owner picks site A's sender and template, and every notice, digest and list invitation of site B then goes out as site A, signed for site A's domain. Reached by the owner of any site through `storeMailSettings` (`PATCH mail/settings`). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:2416-2446`; used by `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/DeliveryWorker.js:646-666` and `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumLists.js:914-930`. Fix: Register senders per site (the operator names the sites a sender may serve, or the sender's domain must be one of the site's `PublicSiteHost` domains), or let only an operator store mail settings.
- **SEC-PFS-100 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A list's staff names any file of the shared import directory: a missing name and a wrong checksum answer differently, and each try hashes the whole file.** `pass@import-dir` is one directory for every site. `importMbox` answers Not Found for a name that is not there and Conflict for a name that is there with a wrong checksum, after `ec.resource.digest` read the whole file, so a list moderator of any site learns which archive names exist (another site's Discourse backup included) and makes the server read a multi-gigabyte archive once per request. The checksum still keeps the content out of reach. Reached by a list's staff (operator, site owner, list owner or moderator, the bound scope's Owner) through `POST categories/{id}/mbox-imports`; the Discourse import into the same directory is an operator's (`ForumImport.js:277-280`). Reports: round 3 `C5.md` (Low), `C4.md` (Info). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumLists.js:102-107`, `:442-453`. Fix: Let the operator register an mbox archive for a site (as `registerImport` does) and have list staff start only a registered one, or give each site its own directory and answer one refusal for both cases.
- **SEC-PFS-101 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A repository's Owner moves it out of a group the site's staff hid, and it is shown again.** A hidden group hides its repositories by the chain (`hidden = !!repo.hiddenAt || ... whole.some(g => !!g.hiddenAt)`), and an Owner still views them. `transferRepository` sets `groupId` and `ownerPartyId` and leaves the repository's own empty `hiddenAt` as it was, so the repository has no hidden group above it any more and is shown at its own visibility, on pages and in git. `hideSmithyTarget` refuses an Owner to show what staff hid, and `forkRepository` refuses a hidden repository; the transfer asks neither. Reached by an Owner of a repository in a group staff hid, through `transferRepository` to its own namespace (when the site lets accounts have personal repositories, the default) or to a group it is a Maintainer of. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyManage.js:864-917`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyCore.js:361-371`, `:418`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyForum.js:304-305`. Fix: Refuse `transferRepository` for a hidden repository (`r.hidden`) unless the caller is the site's staff, as `forkRepository` does.
- **SEC-PFS-102 (Low; reported (likely)). A webhook keeps sending a repository's pushes to the address a Maintainer set after that Maintainer has lost the repository.** Smithy stores a webhook with authorization disabled and records no owner of it, where a mirror is stored with authorization on so that the framework pauses it once its giver loses the delete right. A Maintainer removed by `setRepositoryMember`, who left the group, or who lost the repository by a transfer keeps receiving the deliveries, which carry the last 20 commits with their messages and author addresses; only `check#Outbound` holds them, for a hidden repository, an unmounted site or a suspended owner. `send#GitWebhookDelivery` was not read. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyGit.js:202-232`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyManage.js:572-574`, `:910-917`; compare `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyImport.js:1935-1958`. Fix: Store webhooks as the Maintainer with authorization on and pause one whose maker no longer has the delete right, as mirrors are; or list a repository's webhooks to its Owners on every role change.
- **SEC-PFS-103 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). `process#Inbound` takes the message from its caller and skips the account check without one, and `post#Inbound` posts as the user it is given.** `process#Inbound` is `anonymous-all`, disables authorization and reads `headers`, `fields` and `bodyPartList` from its context; without `emailAccountId` and `emailMessageId` it skips `forumAccount`, so a caller that names it with its own `Authentication-Results` posts as any verified account. `post#Inbound` (`authenticate="false"`) signs in as `input.username` and publishes. Today only the email rule `ForumInbound.emecas.xml` calls the first, and no PASS route, REST resource, transition or tool names either; `C4.md` adds that `poll#EmailServer` runs the rule from a polled message's fields with no account (`crates/moqui-mail/src/inbound.rs:169-172`), where the account check is skipped too. The same class as SEC-PFS-03, which lists `DeliveryServices.xml:242` but not these. Reports: round 3 `C4.md`, `C5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/DeliveryServices.xml:158-179`, `:281-295`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:5254-5264`, `:5608-5645`. Fix: Declare both `authenticate="false"` and refuse a call whose authorization is not already disabled (as SEC-PFS-03 suggests), and in `processInbound` require a stored message and read its headers from the row.
- **SEC-PFS-104 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). `decideReviews` answers a nested failure's first line as it is, past the authority's internal-text filter.** For each review item whose `decide#Review` fails, `decideReviews` answers the first line of the failure raw, where the envelope's filter keeps internal text from the caller (`PassAuthority.js:278-281`). Reached by site or category staff through `POST reviews/decisions`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumModeration.js:1729-1737`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/script/PassAuthority.js:278-281`. Fix: not given in the report (each item's failure passed through the same filter would close it).
- **SEC-PFS-105 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Any caller locks a site's `PublicSite` row for the length of a refused call.** `site(id, true)` reads the `PublicSite` row `FOR UPDATE` before it checks that the caller is its owner (`ForumCore.js:68` `const v = one(S + 'PublicSite', { siteId: id }, staff)`, then `:76`), so every GET or write that calls it locks the row briefly for any caller; so do `setCategoryMember`, `endSanction` and `applyTopicAction`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumCore.js:67-77`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:3184-3186`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumModeration.js:611`, `:969`. Fix: not given in the report (check the caller before the locking read).
- **SEC-PFS-106 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). A message that looks like a delivery report stops a member's forum mail with no sender authentication.** A message to a route whose `Content-Type` is `multipart/report`, or whose sender is `mailer-daemon@` or `postmaster@`, is handled as a bounce before `gatewayAuthenticated` and the account check, and a `Status: 5.x.x` in its body with a delivery's Message-ID sets the member's site preference to `None`, `suppressed`. Message-IDs are 96 random bits, so this needs one that leaked (a notice the member forwarded, or replied to with others in copy). Reached by anyone who sends mail to a route address. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/Forum.js:5338-5343`, `:5673-5713`; `crates/moqui-mail/src/implementation.rs:2203-2211`. Fix: Take a report only at the address the forum mail used as its return path, or only from a gateway-authenticated reporting domain; or count it as a soft signal (several reports) before stopping mail.
- **SEC-PFS-107 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). One delivery queue serves every site, 100 sends a run.** `dispatch` (the job `pass_dispatch_outbox`, every minute) prepares the 100 oldest pending outboxes of all sites in 20 seconds and sends the 100 oldest due deliveries of all sites. A site whose list has thousands of every-post subscribers puts every other site's notices behind its own. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/DeliveryWorker.js:950-985`. Fix: Take deliveries round-robin by site, or cap one site's share of a run.
- **SEC-PFS-108 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). An Owner of one repository takes a group's repository into its own namespace, and the group's Owners lose it.** `repositoryFor(p, "SmrOwner")` takes a role row on the repository as well as one on its group, and a transfer to the caller's namespace asks nothing of the group the repository leaves. After it the group's rows no longer apply, so its Owners lose the repository, with its own role rows, secrets, environments and webhooks. `deleteRepository` already lets the same account destroy it, and the map lists transfer among Owner acts, so this is a design point. Reached by an account given Owner on one repository (`setRepositoryMember`), through `transferRepository` with no `targetGroupId`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyManage.js:865`, `:882-891`, `:910-917`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyCore.js:396-409`. Fix: For a repository in a group, ask for Owner of that group (or of a group above) to move it out.
- **SEC-PFS-109 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). The Branches page gives the numbers of hidden merge requests.** `findRefs` with `refType` `branch` reads the open merge requests by source branch with no `hiddenDate` condition and answers `mergeRequestNum` for a branch, so any viewer of the repository sees the number of a merge request whose text is held. `getRepository` and `getCompare` leave hidden ones out. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/Smithy.js:871-881`, `:901`. Fix: Add `.condition("hiddenDate", null)`, or keep the caller's own hidden ones as `listCondition` does.
- **SEC-PFS-110 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). In a repository a group Owner imported from GitHub, a commit's author address alone makes a signature of the agreement its author's own.** A repository the import made has `importedHistory` Y, and there a commit no push brought is taken by its author address (or a noreply login) alone. A commit in the GitHub history authored with another member's confirmed address that adds that member's line to `AUTHORS.md` makes the member "signed" in the importer's repository. The gate's comment treats an import as an operator's act; the GitHub import is a group Owner's. Reached by an Owner of a group through `startGitHubImport` of a GitHub repository it controls into a new repository. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyAgreements.js:296-300`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyImport.js:287-289`, `:1606-1609`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/Smithy.gitecas.xml:22-23`. Fix: Set `importedHistory` only on an operator's word, or show signatures taken from imported history as such.
- **SEC-PFS-111 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). The agreement check tells any viewer whether an address belongs to a member who signed.** `get#AuthorSigned` keeps `missing` for the author or a Maintainer, but answers `signed` to any viewer for any address: true only when the address is a confirmed address of a site member who has signed, which a signature line (handle and name) does not show. Reached by a viewer of a Smithy repository through the framework git REST's author-signed read with `authorEmail` (`get_author_signed`); the REST resource that maps to it was not read. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyHosting.js:147-165`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/smithy/SmithyAgreements.js:908-921`; `crates/moqui-git/src/service/rest.rs:546-566`. Fix: Answer for an address only to its owner or a Maintainer, as `message` is.
- **SEC-PFS-112 (Info; confirmed (second check)). The Discourse import unpacks the operator's archive with the framework's default limits (4 GiB and a million entries, and 4 GiB more for the dump).** `stageImport` calls `ec.resource.extractArchive(source, staging, {})` and `ec.resource.gunzip` with no limits, so the framework's defaults apply: 4 GiB and 1,000,000 entries for the archive, then up to 4 GiB more for the dump it inflates, all written to the site's private staging. Paths stay under the target (regular files with plain names only). The archive is one the operator put in `pass@import-dir` and named with its checksum (`registerImport` and `stageImport` are the operator's only), so this needs an operator to import a hostile backup, such as one a community's owner hands over. The framework's own row for the same requirement is a Gap (SEC-DAT-91; `resource_extract_archive`'s defaults). Report: the security proofs, PASS part of phase P5 (ASVS v5.0.0-5.2.3); lines read again at mr-mantle `cc085ac5`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ForumImport.js:313-330`, `:366-400`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/forum/ImportServices.xml:15-25`; `crates/moqui-core/src/script/host/tools.rs:727-729`; `crates/moqui-core/src/script/host/archive.rs:216-225`. Fix: Pass `{maxBytes, maxEntries}` and a `gunzip` limit sized to a Discourse backup (a `pass` setting with a default of a few GiB and the expected entry count), and check the staging volume's free space before expanding.

## BOSS access model; Admin, Commons, People, Accounting

- **SEC-BOS-04 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). Unsubscribe and public-share changes run on a bare GET.** The four transitions have `require-session-token="false"` and no `method`. A GET of `/u/<token>/unsubscribe` calls `unsubscribe#ContactListParty` for the token's list, which unsubscribes the party or starts the confirmation mail. The screen comment's claim that a scanner changes nothing holds for the mailed URL `/u/<token>` only. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Unsubscribe.xml:53. Fix: Add `method="post"` to `unsubscribe`, `preferences`, `open` and `upload`. Fixed 2026-11-16: `unsubscribe`, `preferences`, `open` and `upload` of `Unsubscribe.xml` and `PublicShare.xml` take a POST only, so a bare GET of the link changes nothing; test `mr-boss/test/Unsubscribe.spec.js`.
- **SEC-BOS-05 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). A published calendar's password travels in the query string.** The `events` transition is `method="get"` and takes `password`; the page sends it as a request parameter of that GET on every load of a month. The password lands in access logs, proxy logs and browser history beside the token. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/PublishedCalendar.xml:27 and PublishedCalendar.qvue:120. Fix: Exchange the password once by POST for a grant, as `PublicShare`'s `open` does, and pass the grant. Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-boss 8e6fdab9): `PublishedCalendar.xml`'s `events` transition is a POST (`read-only`, `require-session-token="false"` as PublicShare's `open`) and `PublishedCalendar.qvue` posts its parameters, so the password is in the body, never in an access or proxy log or the browser's history; a GET is refused; test `PublishedCalendar.spec.js`. The page still sends the password on each month's load; the grant exchange (as PublicShare's) would need a check through three layers and is left as an option.
- **SEC-BOS-06 (Low; reported (to check)). Accounting separation of duties is a preference, and two bundles hold both sides.** The services refuse an approver who drafted the bill or created the payment run, unless the organization's `allowAccountingSelfApproval` is `Y`. `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` and `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_PAYROLL` each hold `PAYABLE_EDIT`, `PAYMENT_EDIT`, `INVOICE_APPROVE` and `PAYMENT_RUN_APPROVE`, so with the preference on one person enters a bill, approves it, builds the run, approves it and takes the bank file. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentRuns.js:354 and InvoiceWorkflow.js:142. Fix: Require `ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` plus a recorded reason to turn the preference on, audit it, and split the payroll-finance bundle so that entry and approval are not held together by default.
- **SEC-BOS-07 (Low; reported (confirmed in code); fixed). The reset-factor skill is declared reversible.** The skill's actions include a factor reset and "Revoke all access" (sessions, keys, app passwords, SSH keys and phones), which cannot be undone, yet its front matter says `risk: reversible`. Whatever the agent relaxes for a reversible skill is relaxed for these. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/skill/reset-factor.md:8. Fix: Declare `risk: confirm` (as `offboard-user` and `invite-staff` do). Fixed 2026-11-16 (mr-boss 5f04667a): `skill/reset-factor.md` declares `risk: confirm`, as `offboard-user` and `invite-staff` do; test `AdminSecurity.spec.js` "Admin's skills that reset a sign-in or end a user's access declare that their writes need confirming".
- **SEC-BOS-08 (Low; confirmed in code; fixed). request#Provisioning is authorized for ALL_USERS and checks no permission itself.** The only checks in the service are that the change is a hire that asked for an account and that each extra bundle is registered, not privileged and delegable by the caller; a bundle with no `delegablePermissionId` passes for any caller. The request then goes to Mantle as the caller, where an operator's approval is said to decide. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossSetupData.xml:5274 and runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/admin/Provisioning.js:85. Fix: Check `PEOPLE_HR_EDIT` on the change's employer in the service, and refuse a bundle with no delegable permission unless the caller holds `ADMIN_ACCESS_MANAGE`. Fixed 2026-11-17 (commit 8892e5e4e for the docs, mr-boss 41e77918): `Provisioning.js` `requestProvisioning` asks of the caller `PEOPLE_HR_EDIT` on the hire's employer in their People scope (`hrEditorOf`; authorization disabled, a job or a data load, is trusted), and answers anyone else `NOT_A_HIRE`, as a hire not found; a bundle that names no `delegablePermissionId` needs `ADMIN_ACCESS_MANAGE`. Test mr-boss `People/PeopleLifecycle.spec.js` (a directory reader with authorization on).
- **SEC-BOS-09 (Info; reported (to check)). Demo accounts share one password hash and 47 demo loaders are anonymous-all.** 165 demo account rows (144 in `BossUsersDemoData.xml`, 11 in `BossAccountingUsersDemoData.xml`, 10 in `BossZZMarketingDemoData.xml`, accounting roles among them) carry the same SHA hash (the count corrected from 155 by the security proofs, phase P7). The loaders are reached only from `type="demo"` data files, not from a screen or REST path. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossUsersDemoData.xml:47. Fix: Refuse the `demo` data type when the instance is not in a development mode, or mark the demo accounts `requirePasswordChange`.
- **SEC-BOS-102 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A published calendar's password is checked with no attempt limit.** `get#PublishedCalendar` answers a wrong password "The password is not right" at once, and Mantle's `get#PublishedCalendarEvents` checks it again; neither counts a try, the token route counts only refused tokens (SEC-RTE-16), and no rate limit names `/cal` (`mr-boss/MoquiConf.xml:30-32`), so only the general 50 a second applies. A shared-file link's password (SEC-FIL-05's fix) is counted 10 a link and 30 a requester in ten minutes. Reached by anonymous, `POST /cal/<token>/events` with `password`; no screen makes a publish link today (`share#Calendar` with `publish` has no screen caller), so a link exists only from a direct service call or a data load. Not confirmed: the hash type `ec.user.hashPassword` uses (`mantle/work/CalendarServices.xml:414`); a framework-wide limit (D2 rated it likely for that reason). Reports: round 3 `D1.md`, `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/CalendarServices.xml:1143-1154`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/CalendarServices.xml:597-601`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/PublishedCalendar.xml:27-38`. Fix: Count each try by share and by requester before the hash, as `open#PublicShare` does, or exchange the password once for a grant (the option SEC-BOS-05's fix left).
- **SEC-BOS-103 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A project's public request form files the request as the existing person whose email and first name were typed, unmarked (SEC-PSS-28's fix covers queue forms only).** For a project's token, `create#IntakeRequest` (authorization disabled) calls `create#ContactRequest` with `filedByPartyId` and `customerPartyId` null, and that service takes the first enabled person who has the typed address and first name (a private contact included) as customer and filer, the request's Reporter and Customer. The request is then in the project as filed by that person, and the form's confirmation, when on, goes to them.
