SEC-PSS-28's fix marks only a queue form's request `senderVerified` N (`Channels.js:764`); this path sets nothing, so the request shows no mark, and a client contact of the project also sees it among their own requests (`BossSetupData.xml:386-387`). Reached by anyone with a project's form link, `POST /r/<token>/submit`. Not confirmed: how Projects' request screens show the filer (`get#ProjectRequestDetail` not read). Report: round 3 `D1.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:230-250`, `:279-283`, `:950-965`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/Channels.js:764`. Fix: Set `senderVerified` N on a project form's request as on a queue's and show it in Projects, or file it under a new person when the address is not proven.
- **SEC-BOS-104 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Products' guarded-services ALLOW row names no filter set, so finds inside those services run with no organization or private-contact filter (SEC-BOS-01's fix covered ALWAYS rows only).** The artifact group `BOSS_PRODUCTS_SERVICES` names `ironstone.products.*`, `mantle.product.ProductImportServices`, `CatalogRecordServices`, `CatalogCheckServices`, `PriceServices.get#EffectivePriceList` and `ironstone.commons.PaletteServices.get#RecordLink` with `inheritAuthz="Y"`, and gives `BOSS_PRODUCTS` an ALLOW with no `filters`. A matching ALLOW record authorizes the service frame itself instead of the root's inherited record, and `applicable_filters` takes the filter sets of that record only, so finds below run with none; the root's own ALLOW for the same group carries `MANTLE_USER_ORG` and `MANTLE_PRIVATE_CONTACT`. SEC-BOS-01's fix and its test (`AppOrganizationFilters.spec.js:23`) cover ALWAYS rows on screen groups only. `BOSS_PPL_ACCESS_REQUEST_ALL` (`ALL_USERS` on `request#Provisioning` and `request#AccessEnd`) carries `MANTLE_USER_ORG` alone, and because the last matching ALLOW wins, a user in both `BOSS_PRODUCTS` and another app's group gets this record or the other app's `get#RecordLink` row by row order. No unguarded read was found: `Pricing.js:31-33` and `Prices.js:203-210` filter parties themselves and `get#RecordLink` answers a link and an icon; every `ironstone.products` service was not read. Reached by a member of `BOSS_PRODUCTS` through any transition that calls those services. Report: round 3 `D1.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossSetupData.xml:294-307`, `:5445-5446`; `crates/moqui-core/src/user/authz.rs:554`, `:568-569`; `crates/moqui-core/src/user/filter.rs:144-152`. Fix: Put `MANTLE_USER_ORG` and `MANTLE_PRIVATE_CONTACT` on `BOSS_PRODUCTS_SERVICES_READ`, `MANTLE_PRIVATE_CONTACT` on `BOSS_PPL_ACCESS_REQUEST_ALL`, and extend the SEC-BOS-01 test to every ALLOW and ALWAYS row on an inheritable service or screen artifact.
- **SEC-BOS-105 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An Accounting administrator's rows still carry only `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG`, so with no organization active their direct finds under Accounting are bound to no organization (SEC-BOS-02's fix left the entity level).** SEC-BOS-02 named two reaches: `ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` in Accounting's services, and at the entity level the `BOSS_<APP>_ADMIN` ALWAYS rows that carry only `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG`. The fix moved the services to `ACCOUNTING_ALL_ORGANIZATIONS`, but `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN`'s root and area rows still name only `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG`, whose `applyCond="activeOrgId"` is false while no organization is active, so their sets apply to every find under the root and limit nothing, against the fix's stated intent that such an administrator works in their own organizations. The six direct finds sampled under the Accounting screens read by key and pass ids to guarded services. `AppOrganizationFilters.spec.js:11` asserts that every `_APPADMIN` row carries `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG`, so this may be the design for app administrators; whether Accounting is meant to differ was not settled. Reached by a member of `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` of one organization, with no active organization, on any request under `/qapps/Accounting`. Report: round 3 `D1.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossAccountingSetupData.xml:198-201`, `:217-219`, `:248-250`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossSetupData.xml:249-250`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/data/MantleSetupData.xml:491`. Fix: Give `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN`'s rows `MANTLE_USER_ORG` (with `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG` it narrows further), or record in SEC-BOS-02 that the entity-level part stays.
- **SEC-BOS-106 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A holder of an Edit share, or an Edit member of a drive, re-shares it to every user or by a public link.** `share#Resource` needs edit level only. A recipient of an Edit share makes an anonymous `/s/<token>` link of the item, or shares it with any group, `ALL_USERS` included (`search#ShareTargets` offers every `UserGroup`, and `share#Item` tells each member on the FileShare topic); an Edit member of a drive does the same for the whole drive (an empty `path`). The owner has no setting that keeps sharing to managers. `Attack-Surface-Files.md:79` states the edit-level rule, so this is a design point. Reached by a user with `RspEdit` on a shared path or `RdpEdit` on a drive, through `POST /qapps/Commons/Files/share`. Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/base/framework/service/org/moqui/resource/FileServices.xml` `share#Resource`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/DriveServices.xml:651-729`, `:731-751`. Fix: Let a drive's owner keep sharing and links to managers (a drive flag), and refuse a share to `ALL_USERS` or a public link by a holder whose level came from a share.
- **SEC-BOS-107 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An event set to "Only the people on it" is shown whole to every viewer of a calendar that holds it.** EventDetail offers "Only the people on it" (`WevRestricted`) beside the default, but the access rule never reads `WevRestricted`: a View, Edit or Manage share on any calendar the event is in gives view. Reached by such a share holder through `eventSummary`, `EventDetail`, `calendarEvents`, `exportIcs` and `exportCalendar`. Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventServices.xml:698-710`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/CommunicationServices.xml:2429-2439`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Commons/Calendar/EventDetail.xml:274-275`. Fix: Show a restricted event to a calendar viewer who is not on it as Busy only, or remove the option.
- **SEC-BOS-108 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Find a time answers the busy, tentative and away times of any party, in any organization.** `get#FreeBusy` computes each party's busy intervals by `partyId` with no test of who it is, and calendar `CalpFreeBusy` shares are not consulted. The transition answers suggested slots only, but with the caller's own empty calendar they are the target's free time, and approved leave is an "Out of office" event answered as away. Reached by any `BOSS_USERS` member, `GET /qapps/Commons/Calendar/findTime?partyIds=<any>&workingHoursOnly=N`. Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Commons/Calendar.xml:78-99`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventServices.xml:2015-2028`. Fix: Limit `partyIdList` to staff of the caller's organizations and parties already on an event the caller edits.
- **SEC-BOS-109 (Low; reported (to check)). Calendar date ranges are not bounded on the anonymous and signed-in event reads.** Only the date format of `start` and `end` is checked, and `get#FullCalendarData` expands every recurring event over the range; `get#FreeBusy` caps its range at 92 days (`EventServices.xml:1957`), these paths do not. A recurring series expands up to 100,000 instances (`moqui-dav/src/pim/rrule.rs:35`, `EventServices.xml:541`), so one anonymous request with a wide range may build a very large answer; not measured, and the ICalendar tool's own expansion limit was not read. Reached by anonymous through `/cal/<token>/events`, and by a Commons user through `calendarEvents` and `bookings`. Reports: round 3 `D2.md`, `D1.md` (the expansion bound). Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/CalendarServices.xml:285-286`, `:1155-1157`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/ScheduleServices.xml:119`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventServices.xml:133-141`, `:317-318`. Fix: Cap the range in `get#FullCalendarData` (for example 400 days).
- **SEC-BOS-110 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A Commons user puts tasks into a project they are not a member of, or moves a task out of its project.** `check#Project` asks only whether the user sees the project (a General or All-users project of the user's organizations is visible to all), and Mantle's `update#Task` sets `rootWorkEffortId` with no membership check, while Projects' own `quickAdd#WorkItem` needs membership or a project manager (`WorkItemServices.xml:560-561`). A task's owner can also move it, with its subtree, out of its project. Reached by any `BOSS_USERS` member through `Commons/Tasks/List` `quickAdd`, `calendarAdd` and `bulkAction` `moveToProject`. Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/TaskServices.xml:567-575`, `:581-595`, `:867-870`, `:885`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/TaskServices.xml:271-284`. Fix: Ask in `check#Project` what Projects asks (`memberProjectIds`, or a manager), for a move out too.
- **SEC-BOS-111 (Low; reported (likely)). Cloning a Knowledge space carries `allowAnyHtml` to a space the cloner administers.** The framework copies the base `WikiSpace` record whole, `allowAnyHtml` included, and makes the cloner an administrator of the clone; `update#WikiPage` cleans HTML only when `allowAnyHtml` is not Y. Commons itself allows the flag only to `ADMIN` (`KnowledgeServices.xml:1736`), and renders through `m-markdown`, which cleans with DOMPurify; how other renderers show a cloned HTML page was not traced. Reached by any viewer of a space whose `allowAnyHtml` is Y, `POST Commons/Knowledge/Spaces/cloneSpace`. Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/KnowledgeServices.xml:1624-1626`, `:1732-1737`; `runtime/components/base/framework/service/org/moqui/resource/WikiServices.xml:1041-1045`, `:1053-1054`, `:720`. Fix: Set `allowAnyHtml` to N on a clone unless the caller is in `ADMIN`.
- **SEC-BOS-112 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A contact's Requests tab lists every request the contact is on, past the request rule.** `RequestParty` and `Request` have no entity filter, and only `RqtHrCase` is left out, so Sales RFQs, support cases, purchase RFQs and maintenance requests show by name, status, role and date. The Tasks tab of the same service checks each task with `get#RecordLinkInfo`. Reached by any `BOSS_USERS` member for any party they see (`get#ContactActivity` `tab=requests`). Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/ContactServices.xml:659-673`. Fix: Keep a request only when `RequestServices.get#RequestAccess` (or `get#RecordLinkInfo` with `checkOnly`) answers it visible.
- **SEC-BOS-113 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Quick create files a request with any party as its customer.** QuickCreate's `create#Request` passes the request's `customerPartyId` to Mantle, which writes the Customer party unchecked; `raise#Request` (`RequestServices.xml:291-294`) refuses another user's personal contact and a party the caller cannot see, quick create does not. The request then becomes visible to the customer organization's users by the request rule. Reached through `Commons/QuickCreate.xml:82` (`createRequest`). Report: round 3 `D2.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/QuickCreateServices.xml:308-342`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:55`. Fix: Apply `raise#Request`'s party check.
- **SEC-BOS-114 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A payroll editor changes pay rates, structure inputs and tax profiles, their own included, with no approval.** A salary change waits for a payroll approver who is neither the editor nor the person, but the other inputs of the same pay do not: an employee's worker hourly or piece rate (read by the calculation as `hourlyRate`) is created or changed at once, an assignment's input values and elected lines are written at once, and a tax profile (the localization's values, such as an exemption) is written at once. None refuses the editor's own employment (`isEmployee` is true for the editor's own party). The run's approver, who is not the editor, sees the run's amounts, not which of these changed; whether the run's review marks a changed source was not read. Reached by a payroll editor (`PAYROLL_VIEW` and `PAYROLL_EDIT`: `BOSS_PEOPLE_PAYROLL`, `BOSS_PEOPLE_ADMIN`) through People/Payroll/Rates `saveRate` and Pay assignments' `assignPay` and `storeProfile`. Report: round 3 `D3.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/projects/BillingServices.xml:1048-1130`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/EmploymentServices.xml:46-74`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/RateServices.xml:18-80`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/PayrollAssignments.js:767-859`, `:978-1024`. Fix: Ask a `PakPayChange`-style approval for a change of an employee's pay rate, assignment inputs and tax profile, or at least refuse a payroll editor's change of their own employment's pay inputs (as `verify#Qualification` refuses one's own, `Development.js:469`), and show the changed sources on the run's review.
- **SEC-BOS-115 (Low; reported (likely)). The payroll exchange's uploader and reconciler are not excluded from approving the run their file makes.** The amounts of a results import come from the uploaded file and become a pay run in review. Only the user who executes the batch is written to the run's `preparedByUserIds`; the batch's creator and its reconciler, who also sets the signed-off totals, are not, and the run's approval excludes only `preparedByUserIds` and the paid people. Reached by a user holding `PEOPLE_IMPORT`, `PAYROLL_EDIT` and `PAYROLL_APPROVE` (`ADMIN`, or a member of both payroll bundles) who previews and reconciles a file through PayrollExchange while another user executes it. Not confirmed: `executeResults` was read from line 1070 to 1115 only. Report: round 3 `D3.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/PayrollExchange.js:208-230`, `:687-716`, `:1080-1110`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Approvals.js:563-584`. Fix: Add the batch's creator and reconciler to the run's `preparedByUserIds` when the run is made or updated.
- **SEC-BOS-116 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Identity document numbers and tax profile values are stored in clear.** A passport or work permit number (`PartyQualification.referenceNumber`) and an employment's tax profile values (`PayrollTaxProfile.profileValues`, a JSON text of the fields a localization declares, a tax number among them) are plain columns, while `PartyIdentification.idValue` is encrypted. The application shows the number only to the person and an HR reader of an employer of theirs, and the profile only to payroll, so the exposure is to database, backup and entity export readers. The demonstration localization declares only `levyRegion` and `levyExempt`, so no tax number is stored today. Report: round 3 `D3.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/entity/HumanResourcesEntities.xml:107`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/entity/PeoplePayrollEntities.xml:374`. Fix: Mark `referenceNumber` and `profileValues` `encrypt="true"` (the qualification find does not search the number).
- **SEC-BOS-117 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An HR editor grants their own leave balance and records their own worked time with no second person.** `allocate#Leave` posts a grant or an adjustment of any size and sign to each named employment of the policy's employer, and `assign#Policy` does the same with an opening balance; the checks are the editor's permissions and the employer, none asks whether the employment is the caller's own, so an HR officer gives themselves days of leave that then pass the balance check of their own request. The time import records punches for any employee of the employer, the importer included, from text they type, with no approval, where a person's correction of a day needs one. The code draws the line elsewhere: HR cancels "another person's leave at once, never their own" (`Leave.js:2269`), and development and training refuse a caller's own record; the rows keep `allocatedByUserId`, `assignedByUserId` and `createdByUserId`. Whether imported punches feed pay was not traced. Reached by a member of `BOSS_PEOPLE_HR` through `People/Time/LeaveAllocations.xml` `allocate` and `LeavePolicyDetail.xml` `assignPolicy`, and with `PEOPLE_IMPORT` through `People/Time/TimeImport.xml` `previewImport` and `importRecords`. Report: round 3 `D4.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Leave.js:153-179`, `:679-682`, `:742-823`, `:927-1086`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Attendance.js:1071-1076`, `:1201-1395`. Fix: In `checkAllocatable`, `checkAssignable` (with an opening amount) and the import's row resolution, refuse an employment whose person is the caller (`SELF_APPROVAL`), as `decideRequest` and `Development.js` do, or send such a grant to the approval route with the caller excluded.
- **SEC-BOS-118 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An HR editor reaches and changes the private record of any person whose email they know by hiring them.** `planHire` admits a chosen existing person when they are related to one of the caller's employers, or when the work email given is one of the person's `EmailWork` or `EmailPrimary` addresses; nothing refuses a person employed by another organization only, a person with a user account, or a customer contact. `hire#Employee` then creates an employment at the caller's employer, after which the person is staff to the caller (for an HR reader every employment of any date counts), which gives `hrEdit`: the HR officer reads the person's birth date, gender, home address, phones and emergency contacts, and changes their legal names, birth date, gender and home address on the shared `Person` row every employer's records show. One person employed by several employers is intended (`Employment.js:1225-1227`), so this is the abuse of a privileged hire, and it leaves an `EckHire` change row naming the HR officer; whether a backdated, already-ended hire passes `checkDate` was not traced. Reached by an HR editor through the hire wizard (`HireEmployee/Identity.xml`, `ConfirmHire.xml` `hireEmployee`) or an `employees` import row with `partyId` and `workEmail`. Report: round 3 `D4.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Employment.js:1363-1396`, `:1486-1502`, `:1664-1711`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/People.js:310-320`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Employees.js:374-391`, `:541-545`. Fix: Admit by email only a person with no current employment and no user account outside the caller's employers, or send such a hire for approval by an HR editor of the person's current employer, or have `hire#Employee` of an existing person wait for the person's acceptance.
- **SEC-BOS-119 (Low; reported (likely)). The HR officer who entered or edited an employee's expense claim may approve it.** A holder of `PEOPLE_EXPENSE_MANAGE` makes a claim for an employee and writes its lines, or changes a returned claim's lines. When the employee submits it, the approval's requester and subject are the employee, and `record#Decision` excludes only those two and the kind's `selfPartyIds`; the expense claim kind names none, so the HR officer who entered the amounts decides them (the offer kind excludes its drafter, and the payroll configuration kind each changer). It needs the employee to submit what HR entered, amounts cannot change after submission, and finance approves only after business approval; a run with a rule that routes to HR was not traced. Reached by a holder of `PEOPLE_EXPENSE_MANAGE` and `PEOPLE_REQUEST_APPROVE` who is an approver of the claim's step (`BOSS_PEOPLE_HR` holds both), through `ExpenseClaims.xml` `createClaim`, `ExpenseClaimDetail.xml` `saveLine` and `decide`. Report: round 3 `D4.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Expense.js:137`, `:154-161`, `:241-257`, `:361-414`, `:485-522`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Approvals.js:260-289`, `:318-328`, `:1321-1325`. Fix: Give `PakExpenseClaim` a `selfPartyIds` that returns the party of `ExpenseClaim.createdByUserId` and of the users who last saved a line (or keep the changers on the claim), as `PakOffer` does.
- **SEC-BOS-120 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An HR editor of one organization changes or ends the job classes every organization's positions use.** Job classes are shared by all employers, and `store#JobClass` and `store#JobRequirement` check the caller's permissions and nothing of the organizations whose positions hold the class. So an HR officer of organization A renames a class organization B uses, changes the skills and qualification types it requires (which B's skills matrix and staffing read) or ends it, after which no new position of B takes it. The comment says the sharing is intended; on an instance with one organization it changes nothing. Reached by any HR editor through `People/Staffing/JobClassDetail.xml` `storeJobClass`, `endJobClass`, `storeRequirement` and `JobClasses.xml` `storeJobClass`. Report: round 3 `D4.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/humanres/Positions.js:10-12`, `:876-914`, `:926-928`. Fix: Let a class be changed or ended only by a holder of `PEOPLE_ALL_ORGANIZATIONS` (or `PEOPLE_CONFIGURE`) when a position of an organization outside the caller's scope holds it, or give classes an owner organization.
- **SEC-BOS-121 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). "Match automatically" reconciles a payment by reference whatever its amount or status, and another organization's ledger entry for an Accounting administrator with no organization active.** `matchLines` runs the legacy matcher, not the newer candidate rules. With a check or reference number it takes the one unreconciled payment of the bank account whose `paymentRefNum` equals it, with no amount and no status condition (a $1 line reconciles a $10,000 receipt, and a voided payment too), and the reference comes from the statement file, which a payer can influence. It matches ledger entries by the bank's cash GL account, debit or credit flag, amount and date, with no organization, `isPosted` or payroll condition; for a treasury user the `MANTLE_USER_ORG` filter limits that find, but the Banking administrator row carries only `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG`, so with none active an organization-bound administrator reconciles another organization's entry on a shared chart account, after which that organization's own line no longer finds it. The candidate code requires the amount, a delivered or confirmed payment, the bank's organization and a posted entry (`BankReconciliation.js:672-674`, `:747-754`). Reached by a holder of `BANK_RECONCILE` through `/qapps/Accounting/Banking/BankAccountDetail/matchLines`. Report: round 3 `D5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/ReconciliationServices.xml:16-171`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Accounting/Banking/BankAccountDetail.xml:74-83`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossAccountingSetupData.xml:329-332`. Fix: Make `matchLines` call the candidate rules (`find#BankLineCandidates` and `reconcile#BankLine` for each line with exactly one candidate), or add to the legacy matcher the amount and status conditions on the reference match and `organizationPartyId: paymentMethod.ownerPartyId` and `isPosted: 'Y'` on the entry finds.
- **SEC-BOS-122 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Posting locks hold only while the organization's posting guard is on, which is off by default and an Accounting administrator turns off.** `store#Lock` creates a hard or soft lock whatever the guard's state and refuses to relax a hard lock, but the guard reads locks only when `postingGuardEnabled` is Y. It is N unless the organization went through Accounting setup, and `store#Preferences` accepts N at any time, with a reason, while hard locks exist. With the guard off, postings into a locked (not closed) date go through, and the lock screen says nothing of it ("A hard lock never moves back and takes no exception"); closed fiscal periods stay refused. The posting services other than `post#AcctgTrans` were not traced to the guard. Reached by any GL poster or posting service of the organization; the guard is turned off by a holder of `ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` through `Accounting/Settings/AccountingPreferences` `save`. Report: round 3 `D5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ledger/PostingGuard.js:147-153`, `:222`, `:330`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/entity/AccountingControlEntities.xml:17`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ledger/CloseServices.xml:17-74`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ledger/SetupServices.xml:70-115`. Fix: Enforce locks whatever the guard's state, or refuse a lock (and a guard turned off) while the guard is off, and show the guard's state on the lock screen.
- **SEC-BOS-123 (Low; reported (likely)). A payee's name with a line break splits a record of the NACHA and Positive Pay files.** The NACHA entry's name (22 columns) is the payee method's name on account, else the party's first and last name or organization name, truncated and upper-cased, and the Positive Pay formats write the payee's party name into a 30 to 50 column field; no character is removed. A `\n` inside the name splits the record while the length checks pass, and a character whose upper case is longer (`ß`) makes the NACHA line fail its check and the run's file not written. With City Bank CNB, which has no trailer, the characters after a line break can be a well-formed start of an issued-check record for a bank that reads fixed columns leniently; whether a bank accepts such a file was not known. Reached by any person the organization pays in a run: an employee sets their own first and last name through Commons Account `updateAccount`, which `update#Account` stores with no character rule. Report: round 3 `D5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/NachaServices.xml:289-294`, `:321`, `:452`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/PositivePayServices.xml:221-241`, `:269`, `:306-326`, `:332-337`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Commons/Settings/Account.xml:70-72`. Fix: Keep only the NACHA character set (upper-case A-Z, 0-9 and the allowed punctuation) in every fixed-width field after upper-casing, and refuse a generated line that holds a control character.
- **SEC-BOS-124 (Low; reported (likely)). A statement or return file is read whole into memory, and its amount text runs a quadratic pattern.** The uploaded file (up to the signed-in `max-upload`, 2 GiB) is turned into one string and parsed whole before the 5,000-row limit applies. Each OFX and CSV amount is tested with `/^[+-]?\d*\.?\d+$/`, which backtracks in quadratic time on a long run of digits followed by another character, and the script engine's backtracking expressions stop only at the request deadline; the engine's speed on such input was not measured. Reached by a holder of `BANK_RECONCILE` (`Banking/StatementImport` `reviewStatement`) or `PAYMENT_EDIT` (`importReturn`), or a statement file a bank or payer shaped. Report: round 3 `D5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/ReconciliationServices.xml:821`, `:869`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Accounting/Payables/PaymentRunDetail.xml:93`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/BankReconciliation.js:315-320`, `:460-476`, `:553`. Fix: Refuse a statement file over a few megabytes before reading it and a cell over a few hundred characters, and test amounts with `/^[+-]?(\d+\.?\d*|\.\d+)$/` after a length check.
- **SEC-BOS-125 (Low; reported (to check)). Positive Pay generation calls a service the caller names.** After `check#BankAccess` (`runApprove`) and finding the bank account's delivered checks, `generate#PositivePayFile` runs `<service-call name="${formatServiceName}" in-map="context" out-map="context"/>` with any service name the request gives (only an empty name selects a built-in format), as the caller, with its whole context. A screen user thus calls a service no transition of the screen names; artifact authorization still applies to it. Reached by a holder of `PAYMENT_RUN_APPROVE` in the bank account's organization through mr-marble `PaymentMethodFiles.xml:23` `generatePosPayFile` (a shortcut whose in-map is the whole context), and by an `ADMIN` member through the Mantle REST `.../positivePay`; mr-boss's own path passes no name. Which groups reach the Marble screen, and what its inheritable grant permits, were not read. Report: round 3 `D5.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/PositivePayServices.xml:26`, `:41-45`, `:66-72`, `:111`. Fix: Take the format only from `BankAccount.posPayFormatEnumId`, or accept a name only among the services implementing `generate#PositivePayInterface`.
- **SEC-BOS-126 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A bill's approver may be the user who last changed its lines; only the bill's creator is refused.** The separation of duties compares the approver with `draftedByUserId`, which `create#Invoice` sets once. `store#InvoiceDraftItem` lets any `PAYABLE_EDIT` holder add lines and change amounts and only clears `approvedSourceVersion`, and `store#InvoiceDraft` changes dates, term and reference. So a bill created by another user, by a buyer's `import#Bill` or by a supplier from the PASS portal can be re-priced and approved by one person with `allowAccountingSelfApproval` off, and `isSelfApproved` is recorded N; a payment run likewise compares only `createdByUserId`. SEC-BOS-06 covers the preference and the two bundles, not this; the bill detail screen's transitions were not read. Reached by a user holding `PAYABLE_EDIT` and `INVOICE_APPROVE` (`BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN`, `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_PAYROLL`). Report: round 3 `D6.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/InvoiceWorkflow.js:139-155`, `:189`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/InvoiceServices.xml:691`, `:3467-3593`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentRuns.js:347-375`. Fix: Record each user who changes a draft's lines, amounts, party or dates (as People's `draftedByUserIds`) and refuse an approver among them.
- **SEC-BOS-127 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A receivables clerk writes off a customer's balance alone.** A settlement adjustment of type `ItemWriteOff` (or a discount outside the invoice's terms) on a receivable needs only the side's edit permission and a reason, and the credit memo is created and set `InvoiceFinalized` at once; on a payable the same adjustment needs `INVOICE_APPROVE`. No amount limit or second person applies to the receivable side. Whether `design-accounting.md:83` means receivable write-offs to need an approver was not settled. Reached by a holder of `RECEIVABLE_EDIT` (`BOSS_ACCOUNTING_AR`) through Accounting's settlement (`apply#Settlement`). Report: round 3 `D6.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentSettlement.js:218-266`, `:578-600`. Fix: Require `INVOICE_APPROVE` (or a separate write-off permission and limit) for a receivable write-off as for a payable one.
- **SEC-BOS-128 (Low; reported (likely)). Commons' record search names any invoice, order or request of the user's reach by its id, payroll invoices included.** `lookup#ById` finds an `Invoice`, an `OrderHeader` (by id, display, external or origin id), a `Payment` (by id or `paymentRefNum`), a `Request` and an `AcctgTrans` by the text with authorization on, under the Commons root's filter sets only; `MANTLE_USER_ORG` filters `Invoice` by either party and names no `OrderHeader` or `Request`. `search#LinkRecords` answers an invoice's id and `description`, an order's `orderName` and a request's `requestName`, with no accounting permission and no payroll mark; a payroll invoice's description is "Pay of <period>, pay run <id>" (`PayrollObligations.js:324`). Fix: In `search#LinkRecords` keep a found record only when `get#RecordLinkInfo` (`checkOnly`) says it is visible, as the record card does (`ChatterServices.xml:1332-1334`).
- **SEC-BOS-129 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). The always-approve test payment gateway is installed on every instance and can be bound to an organization.** `TEST_APPROVE` (`PaymentTestServices.alwaysApprove#...`) is `type="install"` data, so it exists on a production instance, and `storePaymentGatewayOrganization` binds any existing config with no production or type check. - **SEC-BOS-130 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A Projects manager renames another organization's work type.** `change#WorkTypes` with `change=rename` updates the description of any `Enumeration` whose id starts with `WktpOrg`; nothing ties the type to the manager's settings organization, and the label shows on every organization's time. - **SEC-BOS-132 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A Projects manager grants `SSH_GIT` to any user group, the client group included.** `set#SshAccess` adds or ends a `UserGroupPermission` of `SSH_GIT` for any group but `ADMIN` and `ALL_USERS`, with authorization disabled: another organization's groups, privileged groups and `BOSS_PROJECT_CLIENT`, which `get#CodeSettings` leaves out of the list it shows. Reached by a member of `BOSS_PROJECT_MANAGER`, Settings › Code. Report: round 3 `D7.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/projects/CodeServices.xml:1121-1149`. - **SEC-BOS-133 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). A skill's `risk` never reaches the approval card of a screen write, so no BOSS skill's risk is shown (SEC-BOS-07's fix changes a value nothing reads for these writes).** The card asks `risk_of_write` with `request.target_name`, which for a screen write is `"{location}#{transition}"`, the screen's `component://...xml` location; `risk_for` matches a skill only when the target equals one of its `screens` or starts with it and `/`, and all 24 BOSS skills name `/qapps/...` paths and no `services`. - **SEC-BOS-134 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Admin's Invitations page makes a new invitation link in its render actions, on a GET parameter.** The page's `actions` (a render, not a transition) call `reveal#InvitationLink` in its own transaction when `revealInvitationId` is given, which makes a new token, stores its hash and sets `linkShownDate`. A load the inviter did not mean (a stray link, another site's request) spends the one showing, and the inviter must resend; whether SEC-WEB-04's rule refuses another site's GET of a render with actions was not read (the cross-site case is likely). Reached by the inviter of an invitation that could not be mailed, within 15 minutes of sending, by `/qapps/Admin/Users/Invitations?revealInvitationId=<id>`. Report: round 3 `D1.md`. Fix: Reveal the link in the POST that sends the invitation, or in a `method="post"` transition the page calls once.
- **SEC-BOS-135 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). A published calendar's token and a project form's token are stored in clear.** A published calendar's `publishToken` and a project form's `WstIntakeToken` setting are stored as the token and looked up by it, where `/s`, `/u` and `/i` keep only `singleUseCodeHash`. - **SEC-BOS-136 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Duplicating a Knowledge page does not carry its restriction.** `duplicate#Page` goes through `copy#Page` and `clone#WikiPage` without history, which makes the copy through `update#WikiPage` with no `restrictView` or `restrictUpdate`. - **SEC-BOS-137 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). - **SEC-BOS-138 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). An Accounting administrator lists every user account and user group of the instance.** The `users` and `groups` choice sources check administration in the organization, then find `moqui.security.UserAccount` (user id, username, full name) or `UserGroup` with authorization disabled and no organization condition, 30 a search. The lock exception picker is their use; an organization-bound administrator thus enumerates every account of every organization. - **SEC-BOS-139 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Fix: As SEC-BOS-09: refuse the `demo` type outside development, or make the tokens random per load.
- **SEC-BOS-140 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). The posting guard lets any `ACCOUNTING_ADMIN` holder post to another organization (SEC-BOS-02's fix does not reach it).** With the posting guard on, the posting organization must be one of the user's unless the user is a system administrator or holds `ACCOUNTING_ADMIN`, which SEC-BOS-02's fix made organization-bound everywhere else. No non-administrator path was found: BOSS's journal posting and the other posting callers check or derive the organization first. Reached by `post#AcctgTrans` from Marble's `EditTransaction.xml:26` `postAcctgTrans` (`ADMIN` and `MarbleErpAdmin`) and the `ADMIN`-only REST root. Report: round 3 `D6.md`. - **SEC-BOS-141 (Info; confirmed (second check)). Commons Account signs out one of the user's sessions with no fresh sign-in, and lists at most 10 sessions of the last 30 days.** The page's `signOutSession` calls the framework's `revoke#Sessions` for the user's own session, which asks a recent sign-in only for another account's sessions; "sign out everywhere" (`revoke#OwnAccess`) asks one. The list is `find#Sessions` with `sinceDays: 30, limit: 10`, so a user with more sessions, or an older one still alive, does not see them all to end them. ASVS asks that a user see every active session and end any after confirming their credentials; ending one of one's own other sessions does no harm on its own. Report: the security proofs, phase P7 (ASVS v5.0.0-7.5.2); lines read again at mr-boss `c8c6e27e`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Commons/Settings/Account.xml:46-53`, `:194-195`; `runtime/components/base/framework/service/org/moqui/admin/Identity.js:311-345`. Fix: Page the session list (or show every live session) and have a single sign-out ask the same recent sign-in as "sign out everywhere".
- **SEC-BOS-142 (Info; confirmed (second check)). An html-mode `<text>` prints a value as it is where the qvt branch beside it encodes the same value.** A field's `render-mode` gives a qvt `<text>` that passes the value through `WebUtilities.encodeHtml` and an html `<text>` that prints `${value}` unencoded, so the page `/apps/...` renders in html mode carries the text as markup: a gateway transaction's outcome (`outcomeText`, a gateway's `transactionStatus` while pending) on Accounting's gateway transactions and a payment's gateway tab, and a work center's offline reason (a downtime record's reason text) on Manufacturing's centers. The framework's inline `<text>` never encodes (`docs/dev/JS-Fancy-Templates.md`); the values seen are system words or staff text, and whether every writer of `transactionStatus` and of a downtime reason refuses `<` was not traced. The P7 reader counts 1,032 inline `<text>` with an expression in mr-boss outside `tui`; these three are the ones found where the qvt branch encodes and the html one does not. Report: the security proofs, phase P7 (ASVS v5.0.0-1.2.1); lines read again at mr-boss `c8c6e27e`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Accounting/Payments/GatewayTransactions.xml:166-170`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Accounting/Payments/PaymentDetail/Gateway.xml:101-106`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Manufacturing/WorkCenters/Centers.xml:239-242`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/accounting/Payments.js:164`, `:442`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/ProductionPlanning.js:193`, `:270`. Fix: `${WebUtilities.encodeHtml(...)}` in the three html branches, and a lint that flags an html `<text>` whose qvt sibling encodes the same name.
- **SEC-BOS-143 (Info; confirmed (second check)). A public share's grant travels in the query string of every file URL, and the request log keeps it.** For a share with a password, `open` trades the password (in a POST body) for a grant that lasts an hour, and the page then builds each file's address as `download?grant=...&path=...` (`fileUrl`) for an `<img src>`, a link and `window.open`, so the grant is in the browser's history, in the server's request log (`grant` is not a name `secret_parameter` hides) and in any proxy's log, beside the share's token in the path. Whoever reads such a log within the hour opens the share's files without the password. Not SEC-BOS-05, which was a published calendar's password in the query and is fixed. Report: the security proofs, phase P7 (ASVS v5.0.0-14.2.1); lines read again at mr-boss `c8c6e27e`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/PublicShare.qvue:85`, `:93`, `:204`, `:237-243`; `crates/moqui-core/src/context/identity.rs:4-49`. Fix: Send a file by a POST with the grant in the body (a form for a download, a `fetch` and an object URL for a preview), or put the grant in a short-lived cookie scoped to the share's path; add `grant` to the secret parameter names either way.
- **SEC-BOS-144 (Info; confirmed (second check)). BOSS's staff and visitor uploads are typed by their file name and never scanned.** The `/r` request form accepts a file by its extension alone (`allowed`, a list of picture, PDF, text, office and archive endings), Mantle's catalog documents and the record files panel take the type from the name (`contentTypeOf(filename)`), and nothing looks at the leading bytes or sends the file to a scanner: `pass@scanner-service` is called only for PASS's theme assets, portal media and forum attachments. The framework serves a user file with `nosniff`, an active type as an attachment and an inline one sandboxed, so a misnamed file does not run in the application's origin; what is left is a malicious document passed on to staff and customers. Report: the security proofs, phase P7 (ASVS v5.0.0-5.2.2, 5.4.3); lines read again at mr-mantle `4314cc87`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:879-883`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/Content.js:232-234`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/public/ThemeAuthoring.js:611`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/PortalMedia.js:38`. Fix: Check an upload's leading bytes against its extension where the kinds are known (`/r`, receipts, catalog documents), and send BOSS's anonymous uploads (`/r`, `/s`) to the scanner when one is configured, as PASS does.
- **SEC-BOS-145 (Info; confirmed (second check)). An administrator's reset of a lost factor lets the password alone enrol a new one, and the text-message factor is offered with no word of its risk.** Admin's "Reset" of a factor (`resetAuthentication`, `resetKind` `factor`) ends it with a reason recorded, and its answer says the user enrols a new one at the next sign-in: nothing asks the administrator to establish who asked, so whoever talks an administrator into ending the factor and knows the password signs in. Commons Account offers a text-message factor beside an authenticator when SMS is set up, with no notice that SMS can be redirected (a SIM swap, a forwarded number). Report: the security proofs, phase P7 (ASVS v5.0.0-6.4.4, 6.6.1); lines read again at mr-mantle `4314cc87` and mr-boss `c8c6e27e`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/Identity.js:703-744`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Admin/Users/UserDetail.xml:121-125`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Commons/Settings/Account.xml:338-343`. Fix: Ask the administrator to confirm how the person was verified (a choice recorded with the reason), and have the next sign-in after a factor reset enrol only through a code sent to the account's verified address or a second administrator's approval; add a line to the SMS dialog that an authenticator is safer.
- **SEC-BOS-146 (Low; likely (second check)). An invitation is still accepted by a GET a mail client or a page of the same origin starts (SEC-BOS-03's fix stops only a GET another site starts).** SEC-BOS-03 is recorded fixed by SEC-WEB-04's rule, but `acceptExisting` still names no method, and the rule refuses a GET with no session token only when `started_by_another_site` holds: a `Sec-Fetch-Site` other than `same-origin` and `none`, or, with none, a `Referer` of another host. A link opened from a mail client or a bookmark (`none`), and a link or an image on a page of the same origin, still run `accept#Invitation` for the signed-in user. So the identity operator of SEC-BOS-03 who mails the invited address `/i/<token>/acceptExisting` instead of `/i/<token>` gets the account into their organization when the user opens the mail's link. Not confirmed: that the token route applies the screen's GET rule as an ordinary screen does (as SEC-BOS-03's own entry), and what the inviter can then do to the account; not run. Report: the security proofs, phase P7 (ASVS v5.0.0-3.5.3); lines read again at mr-boss `c8c6e27e`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/AcceptInvitation.xml:35`, `:43`, `:53`; `crates/moqui-core/src/web/mod.rs:658-675`; `crates/moqui-core/src/screen/render.rs:2788-2802` (`cross_site_write`). Fix: SEC-BOS-03's own: `method="post"` on `acceptExisting`, `createAccount` and `confirmFactor`.

## BOSS business apps and the Mantle REST root

- **SEC-BAP-03 (Low; reported (likely)). A gateway's address is checked against its provider's hosts only on a production instance, and private addresses are refused only when `allowPrivateNetwork` is `N`.** on an instance where `production()` is false any `sendUrl` host is accepted, and the adapters call `denyInternal()` only when `allowPrivateNetwork === "N"`, so an unset value permits loopback and LAN addresses. The gateway then posts the API login and transaction key (or the OAuth client secret) to that address. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/GatewayConnections.js:112-124; runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/gateway/AuthorizeNet.js:120-122; PayTrace.js:247, 288; ext/shipment/Shippo.js:186. Fix: deny internal addresses unless `allowPrivateNetwork` is `Y`, and apply the provider host list wherever a payment gateway's remote is written.
- **SEC-BAP-04 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). `get#PartyPaymentMethodInfo` returns the whole card and bank account records and ignores its `partyId`.** the service finds the payment method by `paymentMethodId` alone and returns `creditCard` and `bankAccount` as maps next to `maskedCardNumber`, so a caller gets the stored card number (and a security code still held) and the full bank numbers of any party's method. The `partyId` in the path is never compared with the method's owner. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentMethodServices.xml:17-70; runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle.rest.xml:140-146. Fix: return only masked values, drop `cardSecurityCode` from every output, and refuse a method whose `ownerPartyId` is not the `partyId` given. Fixed 2026-11-16: `get#PartyPaymentMethodInfo` refuses a method another party owns and answers whole numbers only to the owner's user or a holder of `PAYMENT_NUMBER_VIEW` (the owner's decision, mr-mantle 037b4863: seeded on `ADMIN`; mr-boss 824d344f grants it to `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_ADMIN`, `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_TREASURY` and `BOSS_ACCOUNTING_PAYROLL`; mr-marble 5c59492 to `MarbleErpAdmin`; `CARD_NUMBER_EDIT` and `PAYMENT_EDIT` show no number, and `CARD_NUMBER_EDIT` keeps Marble's card number input, an empty save keeping the number), `get#PartyPaymentMethodInfoList` answers masked numbers only, neither answers `cardSecurityCode`, and Marble's form sends the bank number only when it is shown; tests `mr-mantle/test/account/PaymentMethodServices.spec.js`, `mr-marble/test/PaymentMethodForm.spec.js`.
- **SEC-BAP-05 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). A card security code stays stored until an authorization succeeds.** the only clearing read is in the service that marks a payment `PmntAuthorized`. A card that is declined, never authorized, or stored without a payment keeps its (encrypted) security code, which PCI DSS does not allow after the authorization attempt. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/account/PaymentServices.xml:1891-1895; runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/entity/AccountingAccountEntities.xml:1217. Fix: clear the code after every authorization attempt whatever its result, and refuse to persist it when no authorization follows. Fixed 2026-11-17 (mr-mantle 7092a34e): `authorize#SinglePayment` clears the card's security code after every attempt, declined, failed or pending as well as authorized; the hourly ServiceJob `clear_StaleCardSecurityCodes` (`PaymentMethodServices.clear#StaleCardSecurityCodes`) clears a code still stored an hour after its card last changed, which covers a card stored with no authorization after it. Test mr-mantle `PaymentGateway.spec.js` "a card's security code is cleared after a declined authorization, and by the job an hour after it was given".
- **SEC-BAP-06 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). Label fetches GET a stored URL with no internal-address guard.** the label print service GETs the stored URL with no `denyInternal` and no timeout, and sends the bytes to a network printer; `Shippo.js` `download` does the same for the URL in a Shippo answer. Whoever can write the URL field makes the server fetch an internal address. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/shipment/ShipmentServices.xml:1981; runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/shipment/Shippo.js:214-218. Fix: add `.denyInternal()` and a timeout, and accept only https URLs of the carrier's hosts. Fixed 2026-11-16 in part: the label print service and `Shippo.js` `download` fetch an https URL only, with `.denyInternal()` (redirects too), the print service in 60 seconds; left: the carrier's hosts only, for which no host list exists. Fixed 2026-11-17 (the rest; commit 6c112f7e4, mr-mantle 005e7cbd, mr-marble c9afa489): each shipping gateway type of mr-mantle's `GatewayConnections.js` names the hosts of its label files (`labelHosts`; Shippo's presigned storage links at `shippo-delivery.s3.amazonaws.com`, `shippo-delivery-east.s3.amazonaws.com` and `deliver.goshippo.com`), and `mantle.account.GatewayConnectionServices.check#LabelUrl` refuses a URL that is not https and, on a production instance (BAP-03's rule), one on another host. The label print service and `Shippo.js` `download` ask it before they fetch, and so does a third path the review did not list: mr-marble's `getShipmentLabel` (`template/shipment/ShipmentTransitions.xml`), which streamed a stored label, return label or invoice URL through `ec.web.sendResourceResponse` with no https check (403 now). Test mr-mantle `shipment/LabelUrl.spec.js`.
- **SEC-BAP-08 (Low; reported (to check); fixed). The bank file services hold no permission or organization check.** `generate#NachaFile`, `upload#OfxVersion1`, `import#OfxVersion1` and the BAI equivalents contain no `hasPermission` and no `check#` call. Under an app root's inheritable ALLOW any transition that names them runs them for any payment method id. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/ext/account/NachaServices.xml:19, OfxServices.xml:16-53, BaiServices.xml:16-53. Fix: check treasury authority and the organization of the payment method inside each service. Fixed 2026-11-16: with authorization on, `import#OfxVersion1` and `import#BaiVersion2` call `check#BankAccess` with `reconcile`, and `generate#NachaFile` and `generate#PositivePayFile` with `runApprove` (`PAYMENT_RUN_APPROVE`, the payment run approval authority, the owner's decision; mr-mantle d01ddb11, 6a3f41b1; Positive Pay checked nothing before), in the bank account's organization; a payment run's file, the NACHA and Positive Pay jobs and system-message consumes run with authorization off as before; the Mantle REST resources `parties/paymentMethods/{id}/nacha` and `.../positivePay` are POST, not GET (each writes a `PaymentMethodFile` and marks its payments sent); test `mr-mantle/test/ext/BankOutputFiles.spec.js`.
- **SEC-BAP-07 (Info; reported (confirmed in code)). Under `/rest/s1/mantle` an administrator's calls run with no row filter set and past every BOSS sub-area DENY.** `MANTLE_API_ADMIN` is an inheritable ALWAYS with no `ArtifactAuthzFilter`, so `MANTLE_USER_ORG`, `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG` and `MANTLE_PRIVATE_CONTACT` do not apply, an active organization does not narrow anything, and 159 entity methods write Mantle records with no service rule (party roles, identifications, payment methods). The BOSS roots give the same group `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG` and private-contact filters. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/data/MantleSetupData.xml:17-20; crates/moqui-core/src/service/rest.rs:466-471. Fix: attach `MANTLE_ACTIVE_ORG` and `MANTLE_PRIVATE_CONTACT` to the row, or document that another user's private contacts are readable by `ADMIN` here.
- **SEC-BAP-09 (Info; reported (to check)). The Commerce store limit is one entity filter set by one screen.** `commerceStoreIds` is written only by the Commerce root's `always-actions`, and `COMMERCE_STORE` filters `ProductStore` alone and only on Commerce's authorization rows. Orders, carts, customers and stores read through another app root are limited by organization only. Where: runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Commerce.xml:32; runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commerce/Commerce.js:720-731; runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossSetupData.xml:2160-2163. Fix: state the limit's reach in the design, or attach the filter set to the other roots' rows for a store-limited user.
- **SEC-BAP-97 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A mail joins a request by its references outside the queue's organization, and through any other request rule with no sender check.** `referencedRequest` takes every request a referenced message is filed on, and `senderOnRequest` passes staff of the queue's organization for any of them; unlike `tokenCase`, nothing checks that the request is a case of that organization, so one organization's staff mail lands on another organization's case, or on any request. When the rule's value is not a queue, or `mayFile` refuses, `create#RequestFromEmail` falls back to `find#EmailRequest`, which joins the first request a referenced message is on with no sender check, past the rule `Channels.js` states ("a stranger naming one in forged headers joins nothing", `:304-306`). Reached by a sender who works for the queue's organization (or forges such a `From`), or by anyone who mails an address with a request rule that names no queue (a project's intake) and knows a `Message-ID` on the target request; Message-IDs are random, but anyone copied on a case mail has one. Report: round 3 `D8.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/Channels.js:313-349`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/RequestServices.xml:1036-1052`, `:1219-1237`. Fix: Keep only referenced requests that are cases of the queue's organization (staff of another organization held for review); in `find#EmailRequest` refuse a support case (`CaseProfile`) unless the sender passes `senderOnRequest`.
- **SEC-BAP-98 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Customer Service shows a customer's identification numbers to every agent who serves it.** `customer360` reads every `PartyIdentification` row of the customer and returns `idValue` (stored encrypted, read back in clear), and the Service overview lists them, with no capability test (`creditRead` gates only credit). Accounting's finance details show the same rows only to an edit permission holder of the organization. Reached by any Customer Service user, Customers › Customer360 of a customer they serve (any shared customer). Report: round 3 `D8.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/customerservice/Customers.js:252-256`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Shared/Customer360/ServiceOverview.xml:88-100`. Fix: Show identifications only with a permission (Accounting's `RECEIVABLE_EDIT`, or a Customer Service one), and then masked.
- **SEC-BAP-99 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). `FACILITY_ALL_ORGANIZATIONS` opens every organization's Customer Service settings and cases to a Customer Service permission holder.** Mantle's `requireOrganization` and `caseOf` ask `FacilityScope`, whose scope is every organization for `FACILITY_ALL_ORGANIZATIONS` (seeded on `BOSS_WAREHOUSE_ADMIN` and `BOSS_MANUFACTURING_ADMIN`, `BossSetupData.xml:1083`, `:1175`). A user who holds it with `SUPPORT_POLICY_EDIT` or `SUPPORT_CASE_EDIT` saves any organization's queues, routes (with SEC-BAP-81's text), policies, knowledge sources and Support mount, and accepts or dismisses its held requests, through the Settings transitions, which pass the request's organization or queue to Mantle (`Settings/Queues.xml:29`, `ChannelRouting.xml:37`, `:80`, `ServiceDefaults.xml:32`, `KnowledgeSources.xml:30`). Maintenance states the opposite rule for itself (`MaintenanceSetupData.xml:48`). Report: round 3 `D8.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/SupportCases.js:341-349`, `:394-413`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/facility/FacilityScope.js:11-26`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/request/Knowledge.js:82-92`. Fix: Give Mantle's request services their own scope (`get#PartyOrganizationInfo`'s organizations, every one only for a system administrator), not the facility scope.
- **SEC-BAP-100 (Low; reported (likely)). A sending profile's From address and site are not bound to its organization.** `store#SendingProfile` takes any well-formed From address and any `PublicSite`, and readiness calls the sender ready when its domain is one the server signs for anyone (the DKIM conf list or any hosted or shared mail account's domain) or when the relay passed its test, whatever the domain. So a profile of one organization sends, signed, as another organization's domain or as any mailbox of a hosted domain, and points its unsubscribe page at another organization's site. A profile is not bound to a send's approval: approvers see its name, not its From (`MarketingSends.js:421-422`), and a profile changed after approval is used at dispatch. Reached by a holder of `MESSAGE_SENDING_CONFIGURE` (seeded on `BOSS_MARKETING_ADMIN`) through Marketing › Settings › SendingProfiles; the seeded holder also holds `MARKETING_ALL_ORGANIZATIONS`, so the cross-organization reach is real only for a holder without it. The bounce domain must have a hosted `bounces@` account. Report: round 3 `D9.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/party/MessageSendingProfiles.js:91-116`, `:188-203`, `:423-424`, `:447-448`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/marketing/MarketingDispatch.js:534-536`. Fix: Accept a From address only at a domain the profile's organization owns (a domain list on the organization, checked at store and at readiness), and a site whose Marketing or Account mount targets the organization.
- **SEC-BAP-101 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A shipper of one organization buys labels through any shipping gateway of the instance, and a warehouse administrator rebinds every gateway's carrier accounts.** `record#ShippingGatewayRequests` checks that the shipment leaves a facility of the caller's organizations, then accepts any existing `shippingGatewayConfigId` from the request and writes it to the route segment; `gatewayFor` uses it unless the shipment's store binds the carrier to a gateway, and `servesCarrier` asks only whether the gateway is bound to the carrier. `ShippingGatewayConfig` has no organization (a payment gateway's use is bound by `PaymentGatewayOrganization`), so the label is bought with another organization's Shippo token and carrier account. The Ship screen offers only the gateways that serve the shipment; the server accepts more. CarrierTest rates through any gateway with its credentials, the Carriers screen lists every gateway to any warehouse user, and `store#ShippingGatewayCarrier` lets any organization's warehouse administrator change or remove the carrier account every organization's labels use. Reached by a holder of `SHIPMENT_SHIP` (`BOSS_WAREHOUSE_SHIP`, `BOSS_WAREHOUSE_SHIPPING_MANAGER`) by a POST to `/qapps/Warehouse/Shipping/Ship/requestLabels` with `shippingGatewayConfigId=<any gateway>`, and a holder of `SHIPPING_CARRIER_EDIT` (`BOSS_WAREHOUSE_ADMIN`) through `Warehouse/Locations/Carriers` `storeGatewayCarrier`. Not confirmed: whether deployments give organizations separate carrier accounts on one instance (where they share one, this is the design; D10 rated it Medium for an instance with separate accounts); the send path (`Shipping.js` `sendOne`, 597-711) was not read. Reports: round 3 `D10.md`, `D15.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/shipment/Shipping.js:129-145`, `:154-169`, `:256`, `:428-455`, `:465`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/shipment/carrier/CarrierSetupServices.xml:17`, `:108-135`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/entity/ShipmentEntities.xml:672-692`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/template/WarehouseShipParts.xml:44-53`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Warehouse/Locations/Carriers.xml:68-75`. Fix: Bind shipping gateways to organizations as payment gateways are, and check the binding against the facility's owner when a label is bought or rated or a carrier binding is changed; until then accept a `shippingGatewayConfigId` only when the shipment's lookup offers it (the store's binding, the segment's current one, the shipper's `DefaultShipmentGatewayConfigId`), in `get#ShipmentRates` too.
- **SEC-BAP-102 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Every Warehouse user lists the names and ids of every organization's parties through the report and pool policy drop-downs.** The report drop-down finds `PartyDetail` by name or id with authorization disabled and no organization condition, removing only another user's private contacts, 30 rows a query for any term, and a `selected` id answers that party's name; the policy drop-down does the same for every party with a Customer, Vendor or Supplier role. Under authorization the root's `MANTLE_USER_ORG` would limit `Party` to shared ones and the user's organizations', so a warehouse user of one organization reads the names of another's customers, employees and contacts that Commons does not show them. Reached by any `BOSS_WAREHOUSE` member: `GET /qapps/Warehouse/Reports/OperationsHistory/reportChoices?choice=parties&term=<text>` (or `&selected=<partyId>`) and `/qapps/Warehouse/Settings/AssetPools/PoolDetail/policyChoices?choice=parties&term=<text>`; neither area is denied to the launcher. Report: round 3 `D10.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/warehouse/Reports.js:155-170`, `:182-186`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/warehouse/Policies.js:496-512`. Fix: Limit the party choices to parties owned by `_NA_` or the user's organizations, as the root's filter does, or to the parties of the user's facilities' records, and answer `selected` only under the same condition.
- **SEC-BAP-103 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A facility administrator binds any network printer of the instance, and a reprint resends any print job of a printer ever bound to the facility.** Printers are framework `NetworkPrinter` rows with no owner; the Printers screen lists them all and `store#FacilityPrinter` binds any one to a facility of the user's. A reprint takes the job by id and checks only that some binding row of the facility, ended or not, names the job's printer, then resends the job's stored document (another organization's shipping labels with its customers' addresses) to that printer; after the binding `get#PrinterCapabilities` answers the printer's last job and its error text. With a printer two organizations' facilities share, the documents print where the other organization's staff collect them. Reached by a holder of `FACILITY_EDIT` (`BOSS_WAREHOUSE_FACILITY_ADMIN`) through `storeFacilityPrinter` on `Warehouse/Locations/FacilityDetail/Printers`, then a holder of `SHIPMENT_SHIP` through `reprintLabel` on `Warehouse/Shipping/Ship` with a shipment of their facility and any `printJobId`. Not confirmed: how long `PrintJob` rows keep their document; whether printers are shared between organizations in practice. Report: round 3 `D10.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/facility/Printing.js:89-98`, `:123-124`, `:160-168`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/warehouse/Shipping.js:534`, `:556`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/template/WarehouseShipParts.xml:136-149`, `:184-196`. Fix: Reprint only a job that is a document of a shipment the caller may ship and whose binding is in effect; bind only a printer no other organization's facility binds, or give printers an owner.
- **SEC-BAP-104 (Low; reported (likely)). Any staff user books another organization's bookable equipment, the manufacturing class Commons' list hides included.** `book#ResourceSlot` finds the asset by id and requires only `bookable` Y and type `AstTpEquipment`, then makes an event of the caller's and calls `book#Resource`, which checks book access to that event and the resource's policy; with no `ResourceBookingPolicy` and no `BookingApprover` the booking is assigned at once. No entity filter covers `Asset` (rooms are filtered, through `Facility`), and nothing refuses the class `AsClsMfgEquip`, which Commons' list leaves out because Manufacturing schedules it; Maintenance's `bookings` keeps to the resources its calendar lists, its `bookSlot` does not. Reached by any `BOSS_USERS` member through `Commons/Calendar/Schedules/bookSlot` and `ResourceDetail`, and any Maintenance user through `/qapps/Maintenance/Scheduling/ResourceCalendar/bookSlot`, with `resource=equipment|<assetId>` and a time. Not confirmed: whether every organization's bookable equipment is meant to be shared, whether manufacturing equipment is ever flagged bookable, what a booking does to Manufacturing's schedule. Report: round 3 `D11.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/commons/ScheduleServices.xml:78-80`, `:236-252`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Maintenance/Scheduling/ResourceCalendar.xml:45-67`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/EventServices.xml:2111`, `:2169-2178`. Fix: Hold equipment bookings to the caller's organizations (the asset's owner or its facility's, as `MaintenanceAccess.assetOrganization` does) unless a policy shares it, refuse `AsClsMfgEquip` in `book#ResourceSlot` as the list does, and filter Maintenance's `bookSlot` by `calendarResources` as `bookings` is.
- **SEC-BAP-105 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A production run takes as its operator or supervisor any person with any current relationship to its organization.** `store#ProductionParty` admits a person when any active `PartyRelationship` runs from them to the run's owner, whatever its type, so a customer (`PrtCustomer`), contact, representative or supplier's agent is added as the run's `Assignee` or `Manager`. The comment says "an employee or agent", and Maintenance's assignment check limits the same test to those two types. A current `WorkEffortParty` is one of `get#RecordLinkInfo`'s admissions for a work effort (`CommunicationServices.xml:2048-2050`); what such a person, with an account, then reads was not traced. Reached by a holder of `PRODUCTION_PLAN` or `PRODUCTION_SUPERVISE` on a run of their organization, through `Manufacturing/WorkOrders/WorkOrderDetail/Overview` `storeParty`. Report: round 3 `D11.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/work/ProductionOrders.js:1165-1172`. Fix: Condition the relationship on `PrtEmployee` and `PrtAgent`, as `MaintenanceWork.js` `requireMember` does.
- **SEC-BAP-106 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). Standard-cost self-approval is a setting the estimate's own author may turn on.** An estimate's drafter or editor is refused as its approver unless the organization's dated catalog setting `allowCostSelfApproval` is Y, and that setting is changed, effective at once, by a catalog administrator who holds cost approval. The Products administrator bundle `BOSS_PRODUCTS_ADMIN` holds `PRODUCT_CATALOG_ADMIN`, `PRODUCT_COST_ESTIMATE` and `PRODUCT_COST_APPROVE`, so one person drafts an estimate, turns the setting on and approves it; a change reason is stored, no notice is sent. The approved estimate is the margin base, a price-list base and, by the design, the cost of a production receipt (not confirmed in code). The same class as SEC-BOS-06. Reached by a member of `BOSS_PRODUCTS_ADMIN` through `Products/Settings/Index.xml:19-21` (`save`, `store#CatalogSettings`) and StandardCosts' approve. Report: round 3 `D12.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/Costs.js:384-389`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/CatalogSettings.js:148-170`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/data/BossSetupData.xml:994-1001`. Fix: Require a permission the Products administrator bundle does not hold to turn self approval on, or keep `PRODUCT_COST_APPROVE` out of the bundle that holds `PRODUCT_COST_ESTIMATE`.
- **SEC-BAP-107 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A price change, a zero price included, needs no second person, and an imported change notifies only its importer.** A price row, a correction, a successor, a bulk change and a price list's publication each take effect under the editor's own permission; Products has no approval of a price and no floor at the approved cost, and refuses only a negative amount. A "reviewed" bulk change is previewed and applied by the same user (or any editor of the organization), and a selling row of a store is live in the Shop at once. The detective control is the change notice to the organization's other pricing staff, but its SECA skips rows an import writes (`!parameters.catalogOperationKey`), and the import's own notice goes to the importer alone. The design describes no price approval, so this may be accepted. Reached by a holder of `PRICE_EDIT` or `SUPPLIER_PRICE_EDIT` through Products' Prices, BulkPrice, PriceLists and the import. Report: round 3 `D12.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/PricePolicy.js:161`, `:277-295`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/PriceChanges.js:177-188`, `:221-247`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/PriceLists.js:404-420`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/Pricing.secas.xml:4-6`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ProductImport.secas.xml:3-6`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/products/Imports.js:44-59`. Fix: Send the change notice for imported price rows too (one summary notice to the organization's pricing staff), and consider a dated pricing-policy option that holds a price below the approved standard cost, or a zero selling price, for a second editor.
- **SEC-BAP-108 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A promotion editor learns which code texts exist in every other store.** A code's text is unique across every store, so `store#PromoCode` answers `CODE_TAKEN` for a text any store has, and a numbered batch preview answers which of up to 500 numbered texts already exist anywhere, with a count of all codes with the prefix; the screen shows ten taken texts a preview, with no limit on previews. Once known, another organization's code is applied as a shopper in that store's Shop (SEC-PSS-81's lookup accepts the text), unless it requires a party. Reached by a holder of `COMMERCE_PROMOTION_EDIT` in one store, through PromoCodes' `previewCodes`, `generateCodes` and `createCode`. Not confirmed: whether Commerce staff of different organizations share one instance in practice. Report: round 3 `D12.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/StorePromotions.js:555-560`, `:699-714`, `:737-741`, `:751-763`, `:787`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Shared/PromoCodes.xml:115-126`. Fix: Make a code unique within its store's organization only (Shop already matches by store), or answer a taken code without saying whether it belongs to the caller's stores, and count the batch preview only within the caller's stores.
- **SEC-BAP-109 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). The Prices list with no product resolves every price row of every product in one request.** `findPrices` reads the distinct product ids of every `ProductPrice` row, keeps the readable ones and calls `find#ProductPrices` for each, which runs `PricePolicy.access` for every row of the product, and `access` calls `get#CatalogOwnerScope` (and through it `get#PartyOrganizationInfo`) once per row; only the page shown is decorated. On a large catalog one page load does work in proportion to every price row, unbounded, and repeats it on each visit. Reached by any price, supplier-price or cost reader opening Products › Pricing & costs (the default tab), or the Commerce, Sales and Customer Service mounts of `Shared/Prices.xml`, with no product chosen. Report: round 3 `D12.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/service/ironstone/products/Prices.js:120-159`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-boss/screen/Shared/Prices.xml:78`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/product/PricePolicy.js:58-63`, `:340-381`. Fix: Require a product, organization or store filter for the list, or query the rows once with the scope as conditions and a cap, computing the owner scope once.
- **SEC-BAP-110 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). The user who revises a purchase order may approve the revision.** The self-approval test of a purchase order is `placedBy`: who entered it or set it to Placed. Who applied a revision (price, quantity, supplier, ship-to) is not counted, though the revision's audit rows carry `changedByUserId` (`PurchaseRevisions.js:738`), so such a user revises another buyer's order and approves the new revision alone. Reached by a holder of `PURCHASE_ORDER_EDIT` and `PURCHASE_APPROVE` (`BOSS_PURCHASING_ADMIN`, or a buyer also in the approver group), through Purchasing's `startRevision` and `applyChange`, then OrderDetail's `approveOrder` (`decide#PurchaseApproval`). Report: round 3 `D13.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/PurchaseApprovals.js:207-218`, `:267`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/PurchaseOrders.js:514-522`, `:541-557`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/PurchaseRevisions.js:553-623`. Fix: Count as submitters the users who applied or changed the current revision (recorded on the Change hold, or read from the audit log since the previous revision).
- **SEC-BAP-111 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). An approver who re-saves someone else's order-change proposal approves what they wrote.** `save` lets staff or a Sales filer replace another's proposal (kind, lines, price, address); `filedByPartyId` stays the first filer, and `decide` refuses only that party, so the re-saver approves the new revision and, with `SALES_ORDER_CHANGE`, applies it. The quote rules, by contrast, exclude line authors. Whether the seed puts a person in both groups was not checked (they are separate bundles). Reached by a holder of `SALES_APPROVE` who may also file (`SALES_ORDER_CHANGE`, `SALES_ORDER_EDIT` on the order, or `ORDER_CHANGE_REQUEST`), or for cancel and substitute a holder of `ORDER_CHANGE_REQUEST` and `ORDER_CHANGE_APPROVE` (`BOSS_CUSTOMER_SERVICE_ADMIN`), through OrderChangeDetail `saveChange` with the `requestId`, then `decideChange`. Report: round 3 `D13.md`. Where: `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/OrderChanges.js:599-618`, `:644-661`, `:832-835`; `runtime/components/addon/mr-mantle/service/mantle/order/SalesOrderChanges.js:71-77`. Fix: Record who saved each revision and refuse each of them as decider, or refuse a re-save by anyone but the filer.
- **SEC-BAP-112 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A return exception is approved by the person who asked for it.** `decide#ReturnException` checks the permission, the organization and the revision, never the decider against who asked or who entered the return; the request only leaves the asker out of the notice. The approved exception then lets a return outside the window, or with a reason or remedy the policy does not allow, be authorized. Reached by a holder of `RETURN_EXCEPTION_APPROVE` who may enter returns (`BOSS_CUSTOMER_SERVICE_MANAGER` with `SUPPORT_CASE_EDIT`; `BOSS_CUSTOMER_SERVICE_ADMIN`), through `csRequestException` and `csDecideException` on ReturnDetail (the Customer Service mount) or the topic handler. Fix: Refuse `SELF_APPROVAL` when the decider recorded the `RdExceptionRequested` row at that revision or entered the return, unless a policy flag allows it.
- **SEC-BAP-113 (Low; reported (confirmed in code)). A sales approver who edited a team quote's quantities may approve its amount rule.** A quote rule's authors are its owner, who asked for its approval at the revision and, for a line rule, who set a line's price by hand (`priceOverrideUserId`). An amount rule names no line, so a user who raised the quote's total by quantity or new catalog-priced lines, and is not the owner and did not ask, decides it. The map's wording ("other than the quote's owner, requester or a priced line's actor") is what the code does; the gap is the editor of quantities. The seed gives no Sales group both `SALES_APPROVE` and `SALES_QUOTE_EDIT`, so a user in both bundles is needed. Reached by such a user on a quote they see (a manager with `SALES_TEAM_VIEW` sees and edits the organization's every quote), through OrderDetail's `salesStoreLine` then `salesDecideQuote`. Report: round 3 `D13.md`.
